The harness records `aurora_veil` in a state only while a side has it, so no older trace changed; the converter keeps the end tie like the other side conditions and drops the ModifyDamage tie, which cannot apply two reductions to a hit. Mutation checks (no snow test, a recast, Light Clay ignored, a wrong duration, no modifier, a reduced critical hit, a double reduction with a screen, no end line, no end-tie draw, the end order, a wrong side constant, no failure line, an unfilled and a swapped view) each fail a test; leaving Aurora Veil out of the residual's sort list changes nothing that shows (duration handlers tie without a draw), so that one is an equivalent mutant.
5m. **G21, a second data-only batch** (27 moves; after G13). The coverage count of the 226 survey teams of the registry (`docs/research/expansion/data/registry_blockers.json`, the coordinator's count on main 0.31.0) lists these as blockers; each was checked against the pin and the Champions mod (`data/mods/champions/moves.ts`), and a move qualifies only if every part of it runs on a column or handler that exists. The rows were modelled by the generator before (special NONE, no `unmodelled` marker in `pool_tables.c`), so **the tables, the generator and the POOL fingerprints do not change**: the step marks the moves in the manifest and records battles.
   - **Marked, with what each uses** (pin lines of `data/moves.ts`): Sludge Bomb 16920-16936 and Gunk Shot 8017-8033 (a 30 percent poison secondary; Steel is immune), Dragon Claw 4087-4099 (plain; the Champions mod adds the `slicing` flag, which no marked ability reads), Night Slash 12739-12752 and Slash 16837-16850 (critical hit ratio 2), Air Slash 250-266, Icicle Crash 9437-9453, Waterfall 20488-20504 and Dark Pulse 3338-3354 (a flinch secondary; Air Slash and Dark Pulse target `any`), Crunch 3206-3224 and Bug Buzz 1935-1953 (a stat drop secondary), Mystical Fire 12509-12527 (Special Attack -1 at 100 percent), Struggle Bug 18233-18251 (spread, the same drop on both foes), Nuzzle 12841-12857 (paralysis at 100 percent), Fire Blast 5321-5337 (burn 10 percent), Power Whip 13893-13905, Overheat 13110-13127, Leaf Storm 10150-10167 and Armor Cannon 524-541 (self drops after the hit), Dragon Dance 4100-4117, Quiver Dance 14532-14550 and Agility 219-235 (boosts of the user), Will-O-Wisp 20887-20901 (a primary burn at 85 percent, which Thermal Exchange and a Fire type refuse), Accelerock 34-46, Bullet Punch 1991-2003 and Mach Punch 10645-10657 (priority +1) and Drain Punch 4236-4249 (drain 1/2). Flags that the engine does not read (`bullet`, `pulse`, `punch`, `bite`, `sound`, `slicing`, `dance`, `distance`, `contact` where no marked reader exists) are ignored by the existing rule: no marked ability or item reads them, so nothing is hidden.
   - **Not marked, and why** (each stays UNMODELED in the table with its feature): Shell Smash (found by the first recorded battle, not by reading: the pin lists its boosts Defense, Special Defense, Attack, Special Attack, Speed (`data/moves.ts` 16255-16275) and the protocol shows the two drops first, while the engine applies a boost vector in stat order; a per-move order is new logic, so the move was taken back out of the step); Earthquake (`allAdjacent` is not an engine target class: the ally hit, the spread and Wide Guard on that class are new code); Ancient Power (a secondary that boosts the user: the generator has no kind for it); Blizzard (`onModifyMove`: accuracy in snow); Dual Wingbeat and Twin Beam (`multihit`); Feint (`breaksProtect`); Life Dew (target `allies`); Acrobatics (`basePowerCallback` on the item now); Terrain Pulse (callbacks); Clanging Scales (`selfBoost`); Expert Belt (an item that scales damage by type effectiveness: no handler); Sleep Powder (modelled, but the Grass type's powder immunity is engine logic that does not exist); Meteor Assault (G17's class, but its only learner, Sirfetch'd, has no supported ability, so no accepted battle can show it); Matcha Gotcha (spread drain with a thaw and a burn: unrecorded, no battle shows a spread drain).
   - **Evidence**: 8 recorded battles under the POOL kind (`g21_*`, genders stated, seeds by predicate): `g21_poison_agility_nuzzle`, `g21_dragon_dance_claw`, `g21_quiver_bug_fire`, `g21_waterfall_pulse_crunch`, `g21_armarouge_wisp_thermal` (Thermal Exchange refuses the burn and takes +1 Attack from the Fire hits, Incineroar is immune to it, a switched-in Milotic is burned), `g21_priority_punches`, `g21_mach_punch_power_whip` and `g21_crit_slashes`; every marked move is used in one of them (`duoforge.reference.converter_api`). The recorded battles are 133 (125 before) with 720 stops (672 before: six per new battle, none of an ended battle, counted by hand from `tiebreak_ref.h`); 75 moves are marked (48 before). The converter gains one alias for the protocol name of Lycanroc-Dusk (`Lycanroc`, `BASE_SPECIES_NAME`), as for the other formes with a base species. Mutation of the pool rows, 27 edits (a poison or flinch or drop chance, an accuracy, a power, a critical ratio, a priority, a target class, a drain, a boost or drop of one stat, a boost role): all 27 turn `conformance_pool_data` red (22 also `tiebreak_pool_data`). A first Power Whip edit of its accuracy by five points stayed green (the recorded hits and misses do not fall between 85 and 90), so the check was made on its power instead. A short differential campaign (teams of the step against each other and teams A and B, 240 battles, seed 2101) gave 239 PASS and one ORACLE_GAP (`thermal-exchange-burn`, fz_2101_3): a defect of the converter, not of the engine. The precondition of step G14 (a Thermal Exchange holder is never burned) was read on the state before the step, which still shows the Pokemon that a holder replaces when it switches in during the step (a burned Grimmsnarl in that case). It is now read on the state after the step, where the holder stands (a burned holder there is still an error), the unit test `test_thermal_exchange_is_no_holder_of_an_update_tie` has both cases, `diff_random.step_report` passes the state after the step as the converter does, and the battle is kept in the corpus (`fz_2101_3_prefix`, 13 of 18 steps). The same campaign then gave 240 of 240 PASS.
5n. **The Mega batch (#158), nine Mega Stones** (nine stones: Gardevoirite 11 of the 227 survey teams, Abomasite, Barbaracite, Beedrillite, Falinksite, Hawluchanite, Malamarite, Sceptilite and Scraftinite; no new rule for any of them, no table change, no public value; one fix of Unburden that Hawluchanite needs, below). The rule of G18: a stone is marked when the Mega forme's ability is marked, a base ability of the holder is marked, and the base forme has exactly one Mega. The step was found by listing every stone of the legal pool (`docs/research/expansion/data/legal_pool.json`: `mega_items`, the Mega's `abilities_legal`, the manifest at 0.32.0): these nine are the whole list that passes.
   - **The pin.** The stones carry no Champions rule (`data/mods/champions/items.ts`: `inherit: true, isNonstandard: null`), `canMegaEvo` and `formeChange` of the mod are the generic path (`scripts.ts:56-109`, `:182-194`), `setAbility` runs the Mega's Start (`sim/pokemon.ts:1923-1944`). The Mega formes (`data/pokedex.ts`): Gardevoir-Mega :5512 Psychic/Fairy Pixilate (base Trace, marked since AC1: Trace's copy is replaced at the Mega, `ability_now` is zeroed), Abomasnow-Mega :8425 Snow Warning, Barbaracle-Mega :12957 Tough Claws, Beedrill-Mega :341 Adaptability, Falinks-Mega :16860 Defiant, Hawlucha-Mega :13149 No Guard, Malamar-Mega :12915 Contrary, Sceptile-Mega :5058 Lightning Rod, Scrafty-Mega :10476 Intimidate.
   - **Not marked, and why.** Meowsticite: one stone names two (base, Mega) pairs (Meowstic-M and Meowstic-F) and its item row links the first only (`mega_base` is one forme), so a Meowstic-F battle trips the Python data layer's `mega_of` (the replay test stops on FORME_CHANGE); it belongs to the Mega-by-stone model (G23-A). Everything the batch of G18 listed stays as it was (the second-Mega stones, the stones with an unmarked base ability or Mega ability).
   - **Unburden, an engine fix that Hawluchanite needs.** Hawlucha has Unburden and holds its own stone, so a Knock Off at it is refused by the stone (G16) and Unburden's `onTakeItem` has answered first (`data/abilities.ts:5240-5242`: the volatile is added whether or not the item is then taken), a case that G16 left out of its supported setups because Hawluchanite was unmarked. A campaign (120 battles, seed 9181, two teams with the nine Megas against the closure teams) found it: the reference had the volatile, the engine not. Three changes: `dfi_knock_off` sets the volatile before the stone's refusal; Unburden's Speed doubling asks for no item, as the pin does (`:5247-5251`, `dfi_speed_key`); the state invariant allows the volatile with the item held for a holder of its own stone (`mega_capable`) only. And the Mega Evolution ends the old ability first (`sim/pokemon.ts:1923`, Unburden's `onEnd` removes the volatile, `:5243-5245`): `dfi_run_mega` clears it. The public view (`DUOFORGE_POSITION_FLAG_UNBURDEN`, "Unburden doubles the Speed") shows it only while the item is gone: nothing says the quirk, and the Python spectator could not infer it; the harness expects the same (`conformance_compare.c`). CLOSURE and TEAM_C are unchanged (their Unburden holders lose the item first, and no Mega Stone holder has Unburden).
   - **Evidence.** Seven recorded battles under the POOL kind (genders stated; seed 1 except `g24_hawlucha_malamar`, seed 3: the first Focus Blasts miss before the Mega and none after it): `g24_gardevoir_pixilate` (Trace copies a foe's ability; the Mega replaces it with Pixilate: Hyper Voice is Normal in turn 1 and Fairy with the 1.2 after the Mega Evolution), `g24_gardevoir_ghost` (against Annihilape, Fighting and Ghost: the Normal Hyper Voice is immune before the Mega, the Fairy one hits super effectively after it), `g24_abomasnow_scrafty` (Snow Warning at the start and no second snow at the Mega; the Mega Scrafty's Intimidate lowers Abomasnow and Milotic; Weather Ball is Ice in the snow), `g24_barbaracle_beedrill` (Beedrill's Poison Jab goes from the same-type 1.5 to Adaptability's 2 at its Mega; Barbaracle's Tough Claws before and after), `g24_hawlucha_malamar` (turn 1: Malamar's Knock Off at Hawlucha: no boost, no removal, the volatile set, and Hawlucha is not faster in turn 2 under the foe's Tailwind; turn 3: Hawlucha's Mega ends the volatile and No Guard makes Focus Blast hit; Malamar's Superpower raises with Contrary), `g24_sceptile_raichu` (Raichu's Thunderbolt is resisted before Sceptile's Mega and absorbed by Lightning Rod after it), `g24_falinks_pelipper` (genderless Falinks: Defiant answers Incineroar's Intimidate at the start and its Parting Shot after the Mega). 156 pool battles and 838 stops (149 and 801 before the step, main after G21). `duoforge.state.pool_g16` reads the volatile, the stone, the view and the invariant after Knock Off and after the Mega.
   - **Mutation checks.** Fifteen edits, each killed by the conformance, the pool tables test or `duoforge.state.pool_g16`: the volatile set only after the item is taken, the invariant refusing the volatile with the stone held, the Speed doubling ignoring the held item, the view showing Unburden with the item held, the Mega Evolution keeping the volatile, Trace not replaced at the Mega, and each of the nine stones unmarked.
5o. **The residual handler list and the insertion-order refusal (#154, the AWS triage of 2026-10-03).** The engine's residual list is now the pin's `fieldEvent` list (`sim/battle.ts:484-523`): per active Pokemon its status, its volatiles (the duration counters, Heal Block at order 20, Throat Chop at 22, Encore at 16), its item, then Grassy Terrain's heal, sorted once by `speedSort`, whose swaps move tied entries, so every entry decides the pre-shuffle order of a tied group, the entries that never act included. The order in which a Pokemon's volatiles were added is not stored, so the engine lists them in one fixed order and refuses (`E_UNSUPPORTED`, never a guess) the lists in which a different order could change an outcome (`src/combat/residual_order.h`: a cheap test, then an exact one that runs the sort over every order of the volatiles and every value of every draw; `tests/test_residual_order.c` proves it by brute force against its own model of the reference's sort).
   - **Measured rates** of that refusal (battles with a refusal, local runs under the machine lock unless noted): the mirror campaign over the Throat Chop, Heal Block and Encore teams 1 of 1200; the watchdog's first round on 6f47f3e, g9-encore, 2 of 600; a further Encore-team campaign (seed 9900000) 4 of 1200, all four in the DD pairing; the DD pairing overall (the Encore team D against itself) 7 of 225, about 3.1 percent; every other pairing 0, among them D against the registry Encore team (0 of 200); and a registry-mix-size random run (44 registry teams in 500 random pairings, seed 9810000) 0 of 3000, about 51,000 steps. The domain gap of the watchdog run (16 of 28,610 samples) is exactly the two refused battles' samples, which the domain check does not count as agreed.
   - **The reason it is rare.** A list is refused only when a Pokemon holds volatile handlers of two different sort keys (a Protect-style counter next to Encore, Heal Block or Throat Chop) and a group of equal-speed callbacks that draw sits at or after the earliest of those orders and is moved by the sort's swaps. That needs two such Pokemon at one Speed: in practice a mirror of a team with several Encore users.
   - **Why the insertion order is not stored.** The refusal ends an environment of a learner's training; at these rates the loss is the Encore mirrors only, and the storage (a tail revision, the codec, the converter and the view) is a larger change than the loss. HauptSession agreed (2026-10-03).
   - **The fix kept on file,** should training ever use such mirrors: one byte per position in the POOL tail that holds the rank of each volatile class (counters, Heal Block, Throat Chop, Encore) as it was added, which the list builder would read instead of the fixed order; the exact test would then be needed only for lists whose ranks tie. It needs the next tail revision, so it would travel with the next mechanic that needs one.

5p. **G23-A, the Mega of a stone** (the data model of the second Megas; no mechanic, no new battle, and no table layout change). G18 found that the tables link one Mega per base forme, so Charizardite X, Garchompite Z, Lucarionite Z, Absolite Z and Raichunite X could not be marked, and that Meowsticite (one stone, two base formes) was stopped by the Python data layer. This step makes the Mega a function of the pair (base forme, stone); marks and Levitate follow in the next step (G23-B/C).
   - **The lookup.** `dfi_mega_of(species, item)` (closure_member.c; `item` is 1 + the id, as in the member): the base forme's own link when the stone is the stone of that link (`mega_forme`, `mega_item` of the forme row), else the stone's own row (`mega_base`, `mega_forme` of the item row, its first pair). Every pair of the pool is one of the two (Charizard-Mega-X: the item row of Charizardite X; Meowstic-F-Mega: the forme row of Meowstic-F), which the generator checks and fails the build for a pair that neither names; so the tables keep their layout and their columns, and the first-link meaning of the old fields (`forme.mega_forme`, `duoforge_forme_info.mega_*`) is unchanged. O(1), no heap. A Mega forme, an item that is no stone of the forme and an id beyond the table give `DFI_FORME_NONE`.
   - **Who reads it.** The setup and state checks (`mega_capable`, the evolved member's stats and ability, `dfi_manifest_mega_of`: the support is that of the pair's own Mega ability), `dfi_run_mega` (the forme line) and `dfi_forme_of` (the types of an evolved member: it still holds the stone, which Knock Off never takes from its own species), and the data API. The member's state does not change (is_mega and the item decide), so the canonical state encoding and the context fingerprints of CLOSURE and TEAM_C are untouched; their tables are byte for byte the same, and every recorded battle and golden passes unchanged (conformance_pool.h is identical).
   - **Tables.** The five second Mega Stones had the unmodelled feature "second Mega forme X of Y"; it is gone (their callback is the `onTakeItem` of every Mega Stone, data of the link). The handler column of those five items changes, so the pool tables, their hash and the POOL fingerprints change (CLOSURE and TEAM_C do not). 39 items are unmodeled (44 before). The stones stay unmarked in the manifest: Absol-Mega-Z, Lucario-Mega-Z and Raichu-Mega-X bring abilities that nothing models, and Charizardite X and Garchompite Z are marked with their battles in the next step.
   - **Public API (owner's OK, 2026-10-03, relayed by HauptSession).** `duoforge_mega_info {base_species, stone, mega_species, mega_ability, supported}` (5 words), `duoforge_data_mega_count` and `duoforge_data_mega_at` (ascending item id). `duoforge_forme_info` and every old field keep their exact meaning (the first Mega of the forme). `supported` says what `mega_supported` says, for the pair; whether the stone is marked is the ITEM table's answer. The layout dump pins the struct and `python/duoforge/_layout.py` has its dtype. The two functions are bound in `python/duoforge/data.py` (decision 0020's binding of the data API) as plain ctypes wrappers (`mega_count`, `mega_at`), tested against the C values in `python/tests/test_data.py`.
   - **Consumers for HauptSession** (not touched here): `python/duoforge_live/data.py` `mega_of` (:130-138) reads the item row only, the first pair of a stone, so Meowstic-F with Meowsticite and every second Mega are `FORME_CHANGE` stops in `lines.py:316` and a wrong `mega_capable`/ability in `tracker.py:101,498`; the same two-step lookup (the forme's link first, then the item row), or `duoforge_data_mega_at`, fixes it.
   - **Tests.** `duoforge.state.mega_by_stone` (new): every Mega forme row of the pool is reached from its base forme holding its own stone and from no other pair; the evolved member has the stats and the ability of that Mega's row and the HP of the base forme and passes the whole member check; a holder of another forme's stone does not evolve. `duoforge.data.pool_tables`: the lookup for every Mega forme, the five second Megas and Meowsticite by name, the refusals, and the support of a pair over a manifest without Tough Claws (Charizardite X unsupported, Y not). `duoforge.data.api`: the count, the ascending order, the old single link as one of the list, the refusals with the output untouched, the pairs by name, the per-pair `supported`, and the oracle of the setup differential now asks the new functions. Nine mutation checks, all killed: no pair from the stone row, none from the forme link, the stats or the ability of the linked Mega, any stone of any pair counting, the support of the first Mega for every stone, a count that ignores the second stone, an info that names the first Mega, its support from the first Mega.
5q. **G23-B/C, Levitate, Charizardite X, Garchompite Z and Delphoxite** (Garchompite Z 15 of the 227 survey teams, Charizardite X 3, Delphoxite for the case below; on the Mega-by-stone model of 5p). Levitate is the one rule that Garchomp-Mega-Z needs; Charizard-Mega-X needs none (Tough Claws is marked, Blaze is the base ability).
   - **The pin.** Levitate has no callback (`data/abilities.ts:2311-2317`: "airborneness implemented in `sim/pokemon.ts:Pokemon#isGrounded`"; no Champions override). `isGrounded` (`sim/pokemon.ts:2148-2160`): Gravity, Ingrain, Smack Down and Iron Ball ground; a Flying type is not grounded (without Levitate's line); a Levitate holder (not suppressed) gives `null`; then Magnet Rise, Telekinesis and Air Balloon. `runImmunity` (:2245-2263): for a Ground move a `null` shows `-immune|X|[from] ability: Levitate` (:2256-2258), any other immunity plain `-immune|X`. A status move ignores type immunity. Pokemon.ts:2156 and data/moves.ts:16968 are the only readers by id. Garchomp-Mega-Z (`data/pokedex.ts:8183`): Dragon only, 108/130/85/141/85/151, Levitate, 99 kg; Charizard-Mega-X (:123): Fire and Dragon, 78/130/111/130/85/100, Tough Claws, 110.5 kg.
   - **Rules.** `dfi_grounded` (turn.c) is "not a Flying type and not a Levitate holder" (the ability now: a Mega's, or one that Trace copied); every reader of it follows: Grassy Terrain's heal and Grassy Glide's priority, Psychic Terrain's block of priority moves and Expanding Force (G15), the terrain seeds. In the hit loop the type-immunity step has a second branch: a Ground move at a Levitate holder that the type chart does not already make immune shows `IMMUNE` with the cause ABILITY and the id of Levitate (id2 = Levitate + 1), the event that Flash Fire, Lightning Rod and Good as Gold use; no new public value. Gravity, Ingrain, Smack Down, Iron Ball, Air Balloon, Magnet Rise, Telekinesis and Eelevate stay unmarked rows (`tools/datagen/test_pool_rows.py` keeps the guard for them). Levitate becomes an engine row of the generator (handler NONE): the pool tables, their hash and the POOL fingerprints change, CLOSURE and TEAM_C do not; 178 abilities are unmodeled (179 before).
   - **Marked:** Levitate, Charizardite X, Garchompite Z and Delphoxite (manifest; Delphox-Mega has Levitate, the base Blaze is marked).
   - **Who is ungrounded now, and what reads it** (the guard of G15 said no user of Expanding Force could be). Levitate holders of the pool (`legal_pool.json`): Chimecho (learns Expanding Force; Levitate is its only ability), the Rotom formes, Eelektross (Eelevate, unmarked) and Hydreigon (neither learns it), and the Megas Garchomp-Z, Delphox and Chimecho; Trace can copy it. (a) Expanding Force: an ungrounded user has no 1.5 and its target class stays single (`data/moves.ts:4943-4965`): reachable with Chimecho and with Delphox after its Mega Evolution, recorded in `g23_psychic_terrain_levitate`. (b) Psychic Terrain's block of priority moves protects grounded targets only (the pin's hint line: "Psychic Terrain doesn't affect airborne Pokemon"): the same battle, the Fake Out at the grounded Delphox is stopped and the one at Chimecho is not. (c) Psychic Seed and Grassy Seed have no grounded test (`data/items.ts:4903-4925`: `onStart` and `onTerrainChange` ask the terrain only), and Grassy Terrain's heal is `g23_garchomp_terrain`. Nothing is left unreachable, so no narrowed guard is needed; `test_pool_rows.py` keeps the one for the rows that still lift or ground (Gravity, Ingrain, Smack Down, Iron Ball, Air Balloon, Magnet Rise, Telekinesis, Eelevate).
   - **Evidence.** Four recorded battles under the POOL kind (genders stated; seed 1; the Mega Evolution happens and the Mega ability acts): `g23_garchomp_levitate` (the Earth Power of the foe Tyranitar hits Garchomp, Dragon and Ground, before the Mega Evolution and is `-immune [from] ability: Levitate` after it; the sand that Garchomp did not take as a Ground type hurts the Dragon-only Mega), `g23_garchomp_terrain` (Grassy Terrain heals the damaged Garchomp at the end of the turn before its Mega Evolution and not after it; the Knock Off of Rillaboom finds its own stone, and Rough Skin hurts Rillaboom), `g23_charizard_x` (Shadow Claw before and after the Mega Evolution: Tough Claws; the Low Kick of the foe is 80 power on the base forme, 90.5 kg, and 100 on the Mega, 110.5 kg). `g23_psychic_terrain_levitate` (Psychic Terrain by Indeedee-F against Chimecho and Delphox with Delphoxite: in turn 1 the Fake Out at the grounded Delphox is stopped and the one at Chimecho hits; Delphox's Expanding Force is a boosted spread move before its Mega Evolution and a single-target move without the 1.5 after it, as Chimecho's always is). 160 pool battles and 858 stops (156 and 838 before). The conformance harness reads the Mega of a member through `dfi_mega_of` now (it used the single link). A differential campaign under the machine lock (120 battles, seed 9231, a team with Garchomp and Charizard and their stones, Tyranitar and Milotic against a Grassy Surge team and the closure teams): 120 PASS.
   - **Mutation checks.** Seven edits, each killed by the conformance or the pool tables test: `dfi_grounded` ignoring Levitate, no Ground immunity for Levitate, the immunity without its ability line, Levitate stopping every type of move, and each of the three marks removed.
5r. **G20, Spiky Shield and tail rev 3** (the second half of G20; new state, so its own PR). Spiky Shield (data/moves.ts:17532-17584, Champions: only the PP changes) is Protect with a contact punishment: the same `onPrepareHit` (priority +4, fails unless an action is pending, the shared stall counter), the same `onHit`, `-singleturn|X|move: Protect` (Protect itself prints `-singleturn|X|Protect`), and an `onTryHit` that stops every move with the protect flag (status moves included) with `-activate|X|move: Protect` and, when the move makes contact, `this.damage(source.baseMaxhp / 8, source, target)`: floor(max HP / 8) of the attacker, at least 1, `-damage|attacker|hp|[from] Spiky Shield|[of] holder`.
   - **Marked.** Spiky Shield only (Chesnaught, whose Overgrow is marked, plays it; Glimmora's abilities are not marked). **Baneful Bunker** (poison on a contact attacker) and **King's Shield** (-1 Attack, status moves pass) stay unmarked: their only learners, Toxapex (Merciless, Limber, Regenerator) and Aegislash (Stance Change), have no supported ability, so no accepted battle could use them and nothing could record them; Silk Trap, Burning Bulwark and Obstruct are not in the Champions pool, and Quick Guard and Endure are out of this step. A Protect-variant row that gets a player later needs only its handler and a value of `protect_kind`, no layout change.
   - **State (tail rev 3, 0x0303).** The variant has to survive a snapshot, because a PIVOT boundary inside the turn (a foe's U-turn after the shield went up) is a boundary where a state is encoded, and the flags byte of the slot is full. HauptSession's decision: tail rev 3 defines the byte at +26 of a position as `protect_kind` (rev 2 reserved it), the schema is 0x0303 (same size, 1257 bytes), a decoder accepts exactly that triple and refuses 0x0203 like it refuses 0x0103, the other reserves stay documented as unassigned (43 of the 248), CLOSURE and TEAM_C bytes and digests do not change. In memory a position gets an always-zero pad byte (its 27 data bytes with two u16). Internal: no public constant, no version bump by itself.
   - **Rules of the turn.** `dfi_run_protect` takes the variant and stores it in the user's tail with the volatile; the residual clears it with the volatile. After the `-activate` line of a stopped contact move the punishment runs, also for an attacker that it knocks out. Two holders of a punishing variant stopping one contact spread move would show their lines in the speed order of one TryHit event (a tie draws): `E_UNSUPPORTED`, never a guess. Outrage's lock reset is not modelled (no marked move locks).
   - **Event (additional use of an existing value, agreed with the coordinator).** The damage is `DAMAGE` with cause `DUOFORGE_CAUSE_MOVE`, the move in `id2` and the holder in `other`, as Rocky Helmet uses `CAUSE_ITEM`; documented at the constant in `duoforge.h`. No new cause, no new draw site.
   - **Tables.** The special id SPIKY_SHIELD (28) is new, so UNMODELED moves to 29 (the generator checks the whole condition text and that the other fields are Protect's).
   - **Evidence.** Three recorded battles under the POOL kind (genders stated; seeds picked by predicates): `g20_spiky_shield_a` (a contact move stopped and punished, a spread move that is not contact stopped without damage, Protect then Spiky Shield against the shared stall counter, a Spiky Shield that fails its roll), `_ko` (the punishment knocks the attacker out inside the hit), `_pivot` (a foe's U-turn pivots mid-turn while the shield is up, and the next contact hit of the turn is punished after the replacement). `duoforge.state.pool_g20_protect` compares protect_kind of every position after every step against rows that `test_trace_to_c.py` derives from the protocol lines, and round-trips the state through its encoding at every boundary; `duoforge.state.pool_tail` has the byte sweep, the schema tests (0x0303 accepted, 0x0203 and 0x0103 refused) and the invariants. The harness converter reads the volatile `spikyshield` as Protect's bit. 144 pool battles and 771 stops (counted by hand). Mutation checks (no punishment, a sixth, no contact test, plain Protect punishing, the variant never stored or never cleared, no cause, the holder swapped, the variant not encoded or not decoded, rev 2 accepted, no invariant, no pad check) each fail a test; the minimum of 1 is an equivalent mutant (a member has at least 8 HP) and the two-punishers `E_UNSUPPORTED` has no battle (no test reaches it). A campaign under the machine lock (120 battles, seed 2002, `team_spiky_shield.txt`) was 120 PASS under POOL, 236 Spiky Shield uses in 66 battles.
5s. **G22, six abilities** (Sand Rush 14 of the 227 survey teams, Cursed Body 13, Inner Focus 12, Swift Swim, Liquid Voice, Slush Rush and Chlorophyll: the cheap ability batch of the blocker list; the batch rule of 2026-10-03: small mechanics in one PR). The turn code runs all six by id, so they are `ENGINE_ROWS` of the generator (handler column NONE, the POOL fingerprints change with the table hash; no family, no new handler id, no public value, no state, no view change). Cursed Body stays UNMODELED and is out of this batch: see the last point.
   - **The pin** (the Champions mod has no entry of any of the six: `data/mods/champions/abilities.ts` is hashed by the generator and has none of these ids). **Sand Rush** `data/abilities.ts:3974-3987`: `onModifySpe` is `chainModify(2)` while `field.isWeather('sandstorm')`, and `onImmunity` returns false for the type `'sandstorm'`, so its holder takes no Sandstorm damage (`runStatusImmunity`, `sim/battle.ts:2116`: a Weather effect on a target that fails the immunity does no damage; the Sand Rush holders of the pool are Excadrill and Lycanroc, which the types already protect, and Houndstone, a Ghost, which only the ability protects). **Swift Swim** `:4808-4818` (`pokemon.effectiveWeather()` is `raindance` or `primordialsea`), **Slush Rush** `:4347-4357` (`isWeather(['hail', 'snowscape'])`) and **Chlorophyll** `:512-522` (`sunnyday` or `desolateland`) are the same `chainModify(2)`. Hail is not in the format and the Primal weathers are not states of the engine, so each reads exactly one state weather (rain, snow, sun; sand for Sand Rush). `Pokemon.effectiveWeather` (`sim/pokemon.ts:2190-2210`) ignores sun and rain for an Utility Umbrella holder (the item is not in the pool) and `Field.effectiveWeather` returns nothing under Cloud Nine or Air Lock (Cloud Nine is unmarked, Air Lock has no row; `tests/test_pool_weather.c` and `pool_families.js` pin both). **Inner Focus** `:2153-2167`, `flags: {breakable: 1}`: `onTryAddVolatile` returns null for `flinch`, so the volatile is not added and nothing is shown (the secondary's roll is drawn before, one `SECONDARY` draw per hit target as for any secondary); `onTryBoost` deletes `boost.atk` when `effect.name === 'Intimidate'` and shows `-fail|holder|unboost|atk|[from] ability: Inner Focus|[of] holder`. `Battle.boost` (`sim/battle.ts:2020-2088`) caps the change first (`getCappedBoost`: a drop at -6 is 0 and is no `boost.atk`) and runs TryBoost before the loop, so the engine reads the capped delta; Contrary reverses before the cap. Intimidate (`:2193-2212`) names itself as the effect, also when Trace copied it. No other effect is stopped (Parting Shot lowers Attack and shows `-unboost`). **Liquid Voice** `:2416-2427`: `onModifyType` (priority -1) makes a move with `flags.sound` Water (not for a Dynamaxed user). The turn code reads the generated `flags2` sound bit, so the whole pool's sound moves are covered by the one rule (25 moves have the flag; the modelled ones are Hyper Voice, Snarl, Psychic Noise, Parting Shot and the sound-flag status moves that exist as rows); the changed type reaches the immunity, STAB, weather and resist-berry steps through `move_type` (data/mods/champions has no `onModifyType` of its own).
   - **Order of the Speed abilities.** `dfi_speed_key` multiplies the chain with the others (Tailwind, Choice Scarf, Unburden: every factor is an exact multiple of 4096, so the order of the chain is free and one rounding follows; paralysis comes last). Only a standing holder counts: a fainted one has its raw Speed, the rule of `dfi_move_priority` (#126; `ignoringAbility` is true for a Pokemon that is not active). `tools/datagen/pool_families.js` `ENGINE_ORDER` now names the four abilities as the modelled rows with a Speed callback, so a fifth cannot enter without this function being read. The speed of the weather changes from the action that sets it (Battle.updateSpeed after each action, as for Tailwind); the residual order (`dfi_residual_events`) reads `speed_seen` and so the same key.
   - **Event.** The Inner Focus line is a `DUOFORGE_EVENT_FAIL` with `cause` ABILITY, `id2` the ability + 1 and `other` the holder (the converter, `tools/reference/trace_to_c.py`, maps exactly `-fail|X|unboost|atk|[from] ability: Inner Focus|[of] X` and refuses every other `unboost` line). No new constant: a documented use of fields that the event already has (`include/duoforge/duoforge.h`).
   - **Flower Veil.** Both Flower Veil and Inner Focus delete an Intimidate drop through TryBoost, and which runs first depends on the Speed of the two holders (a tie is a shuffle). They meet only on a Grass type with Inner Focus on a side with Flower Veil: no Inner Focus holder of the pool is a Grass type (`pool_families.js` `checkG22`).
   - **Tables.** The six abilities lose their feature list and take handler NONE: the unmodelled abilities go from 179 to 173; the POOL table hash, the generated file hashes and the two fingerprints are repinned (decision 0015 section 2).
   - **Evidence.** Eight recorded battles under the POOL kind (genders stated, seeds picked where a roll mattered): `g22_swift_swim` (no rain: Raichu first; the rain comes in with Politoed, Basculegion moves before it in the same turn; Kingambit's Sucker Punch knocks Basculegion out and its queued action, priced at its raw Speed, ties Politoed's: a `SPEED_TIE` draw of the queue site), `g22_sand_rush` (Houndstone sets the sandstorm, outpaces a 130-Speed Raichu with Shadow Ball, and neither Sand Rush holder takes sand damage while Raichu and Milotic do), `g22_slush_rush` (Beartic after Milotic on the turn of its Snowscape, before it on the next), `g22_chlorophyll` (Drought comes in with a switch: Venusaur, 100 raw, and Whimsicott, 136, move before the 178-Speed Raichu that outpaced them without the sun; Tailwind on top later, x4), `g22_foreign_weather` (the four abilities in the sun and the snow that are not theirs: nothing doubles), `g22_speed_tie_snow` (Beartic's 140 in snow ties Sneasler's raw 140: the queue ties and the two Leftovers heals of the residual are shuffles, checked against the residual list of #154), `g22_inner_focus` (Intimidate at the start and at a switch-in: `-fail` for Dragonite, `-unboost` for Raichu; Fake Out and a 30 percent Rock Slide flinch Raichu and never Dragonite; Parting Shot lowers its Attack) and `g22_liquid_voice` (Psychic Noise hits a Dark type, Hyper Voice a Ghost, both as Water with the rain; Moonblast stays Fairy). `duoforge.reference.conformance_pool_data` replays all of them with the reference's draws (every HP, line and draw), `duoforge.reference.tiebreak_pool_data` with the reference's tiebreak; `tools/reference/test_trace_to_c.py` derives the effect of each ability from the protocol lines alone, and `duoforge.state.pool_g22` checks the tie of the fainted Swift Swim holder's action (the tape without that entry fails exactly there) and the Intimidate drop that the cap already took to nothing (a hand-made state: no battle reaches -6 Attack on an Inner Focus holder). Mutation checks, the campaign and the counts are in the PR text.
   - **Cursed Body is not in this batch.** `data/abilities.ts:784-797`: `onDamagingHit` (not at a Max or future move or Struggle, not while the attacker is Disabled) with `randomChance(3, 10)` adds the `disable` volatile (`data/moves.ts:3648-3716`) to the attacker: 5 turns (4 when the attacker has not moved or is the active Pokemon), `-start|X|Disable|Move|[from] ability: Cursed Body|[of] holder`, the onDisableMove lock of the request, `cant|X|Disable|Move`, the end in the residual at order 17 and `-end`. That is new state use (`disable_slot` and `disable_turns` of the tail), a request lock, a view field and at least two new public values (a volatile id and a cause for the `cant`), and a hook in the DamagingHit loop, so it is a PR of its own (owner rule of 2026-10-03: new state or a new public value goes in a separate PR).
5t. **G28, a move-rules batch** (six moves and one item; the first step after the AWS triage that adds engine rules for several moves at once). The step marks Shell Smash, Acrobatics, Blizzard, Ancient Power, Feint and Earthquake, and the item Expert Belt, each from the pin and the Champions mod (`data/mods/champions` overrides none of them). No public value, no new event, no view change: Feint's line is the generic ACTIVATE event with the move as its cause, Ancient Power's boosts are a self change with no line of their own. Tables: `SHELL_SMASH`, `ACROBATICS`, `BLIZZARD` and `FEINT` are new named handler ids 29 to 32 (`G28_HANDLERS` of the generator; UNMODELED moves to 33 (Spiky Shield of G20 is 28)), the boost-role column has the new value `SECONDARY_SELF` (5, internal, `pool_tables.h`) and the secondary kind `SECONDARY_SELF_BOOST` (7), the target class allAdjacent (11) is now an engine target (`ENGINE_TARGETS`), and Expert Belt is an `ENGINE_ROWS` item, so the POOL table hash and the fingerprints change as for every pool step.
   - **The pin.** `data/moves.ts`: Shell Smash 16255-16275 (`boosts` in the order def, spd, atk, spa, spe), Acrobatics 117-141 (`basePowerCallback`: doubled while `!pokemon.item`), Blizzard 1491-1510 (accuracy 70, `onModifyMove` sets `accuracy = true` in snow, 10 percent freeze, allAdjacentFoes), Ancient Power 396-418 (secondary, chance 10, `self: { boosts: {atk, def, spa, spd, spe: 1} }`), Feint (priority 2, `breaksProtect`, no protect flag), Earthquake 4390 (allAdjacent; Grassy Terrain's `onModifyDamage` 7692-7703 halves earthquake, bulldoze and magnitude at a grounded target), `data/items.ts` Expert Belt 1901-1914 (`onModifyDamage` 4915/4096 when `target.getMoveHitData(move).typeMod > 0`), `sim/battle-actions.ts` `hitStepBreakProtect` 755-780 (after the accuracy step, 568-571), `sim/pokemon.ts` `getMoveTargets` 809-817 (allies first, then foes). The generator reads the callbacks as `G28_FACTS` and `G28_ITEM_FACTS` (whitespace aside) and `tools/datagen/pool_families.js` calls the pinned handlers (`checkG28`).
   - **Shell Smash** changes the stats in the order of its entry: a pass over the stats that fall (Defense, Special Defense), then the ones that rise (Attack, Special Attack, Speed): `negatives_first` of the boost path, the stat order within a pass is the table's. A White Herb then restores the lowered stats after the last change (recorded). G21 had unmarked it for this event order.
   - **Acrobatics** reads the item the user holds now (`item_now`): doubled to 110 with no item, also when it was knocked off, eaten or used up. **Blizzard** never misses in snow (`base_accuracy` 0 in the weather, the accuracy draw is not made) and keeps 70 otherwise. **Ancient Power** gives the user all five stats +1 when its 10 percent secondary roll succeeds. **Feint** removes the Protect volatile of every target it hits and the Wide Guard of the target's whole side (Quick Guard, Crafty Shield and Mat Block are not marked); when anything broke it prints `-activate|target|move: Feint` and deletes the stall volatile of that target (its chain and stall counter go to 0 even if it did not protect; a partner that set the guard keeps its own); nothing breaks at a miss (Feint's accuracy is 100, so the break-at-a-miss mutation is equivalent in every recorded battle).
   - **Earthquake** is allAdjacent: `getMoveTargets` takes the ally first if it stands, then the foes, each answers for itself (a Flying foe is immune, Wide Guard of a side stops it at that side's targets, the ally's own protection counts), the spread modifier 0.75 applies when more than one target is left, and the move line carries `[spread]`. **Grassy Terrain halves it** at a grounded target (a Ground move is not a Grass move, so the terrain's two branches never both apply); the halving is the first branch of the same handler, chained with the other modifiers. This was found while writing the battle (`g28_earthquake_ally`); no earlier step had a Ground spread. Bulldoze and Magnitude have the same halving at the pin; Bulldoze is data-modelled now (allAdjacent) and stays unmarked (the engine's check names Earthquake alone).
   - **Expert Belt**: 4915/4096 in the ModifyDamage chain when the type effectiveness is above neutral. The ModifyDamage chain of three modifiers with 4915, 2048 and 2732 gives the same result in every order (checked by script), so Expert Belt joins the chain as one more modifier; the existing guard on four modifiers stays.
   - **What became data-modelled but stays unmarked** (the generator now reads their rows, so they leave the UNMODELED list without being marked): the nine allAdjacent moves other than Earthquake (Boomburst, Brutal Swing, Bulldoze, Discharge, Lava Plume, Parabolic Charge, Petal Blizzard, Sludge Wave, Surf; Earthquake itself is marked) and the nine secondary self-boost moves (Aqua Step, Charge Beam, Fiery Dance, Flame Charge, Meteor Mash, Psyshield Bash, Steel Wing, Torch Song, Trailblaze). UNMODELED moves 299 to 276, items 43 (after G23-A) to 38. A later step marks them with their own battles (`test_pool_rows.py` still pins what nothing reads).
   - **The spectator fold of Feint** (`python/duoforge_live`, its own commit, reviewed by M11): `duoforge.python.replay` stopped on `-activate move: Feint` (`lines.Stop: line:-activate move: Feint`, found on `g28_feint_protect`). `lines.py` folds the line; `tracker.py` clears, on the converter's ACTIVATE event with the cause MOVE and the Feint id, the target's Protect flag, chain and stall and its open `guard_undo`, and removes the Wide and Quick Guard markers of the target's side from the turn's single-turn set (the markers are kept per side for this: `_guards`), as `hitStepBreakProtect` does; a partner's chain stays.
   - **Evidence**: eleven recorded POOL battles (genders stated, seeds by predicate): `g28_shell_smash_order` (the unboosts before the boosts; a second Shell Smash at +6), `g28_shell_smash_white_herb`, `g28_acrobatics_item` (the same Acrobatics at the same target with Leftovers and, after a Knock Off took them, without), `g28_blizzard_snow` (a spread hit at both foes that never misses), `g28_blizzard_no_snow` (a miss), `g28_ancient_power` (the five rises), `g28_feint_protect` (Feint at a Protect, the next Protect of the target needs no roll, Feint at a Wide Guard of the side, and at a target that had both), `g28_feint_partner_guard` (Feint at a target that did not protect while its partner's Wide Guard is up: the line comes from the side condition alone, and the partner's chain stays: its next Protect is rolled and fails), `g28_earthquake_ally` (the ally Milotic hit first, a Flying foe immune, Grassy Terrain's halving, `[spread]` of all three), `g28_earthquake_wide_guard` (the guarded foes unhurt, the ally hurt), `g28_expert_belt` (super effective at Milotic, neutral at Incineroar). 183 pool battles and 992 stops (172 and 926 before; 11 battles with six stops each, counted by hand from `tiebreak_ref.h`: 66 added, none removed), 85 marked moves (79 before), the generator and row tests, `duoforge.reference.conformance_pool_data` and `tiebreak_pool_data`. Mutation checks (sixteen, each one edit of src/combat/turn.c, rebuilt and run against conformance_pool_data, tiebreak_pool_data, pool_terrain, the family test and the corpus): thirteen make conformance_pool_data fail (Shell Smash in table order, the only one that tiebreak_pool_data survives; Acrobatics never or always doubled; Blizzard rolling in snow or never missing without it; Feint breaking nothing or keeping the stall counter; no Ancient Power boost; Earthquake sparing the ally; no Grassy halving; Wide Guard not stopping Earthquake; Expert Belt absent, on a neutral hit, or 1.1) and three survive: Feint breaking at a miss (equivalent: its accuracy is 100 and no recorded Feint misses), Earthquake not tagged spread (the tag is read only in the AfterMove step and only when the user fainted from the move, which Earthquake (no recoil, no contact) cannot cause: an equivalent mutant for now), Expert Belt on a neutral hit (survived the first ten battles, killed once g28_expert_belt had a neutral target). Campaign: two new teams (H: Blastoise, Staraptor, Garchomp, Abomasnow, Infernape, Aggron; I: Rillaboom, Garchomp, Raichu, Pelipper, Meowscarada, Blastoise with White Herb) against each other, themselves and teams A and B, seed 2801, 240 battles under the machine lock: 240 PASS, 880 sampled requests all agreed. Not covered by a recorded battle: Quick Guard (not marked), Shell Smash under Contrary, Feint at a spread target with a partner's guard on the other side.
5u. **G30, powder, Fangs, Solar Beam and four abilities** (the biggest blockers of the 2026-10-03 survey of the Reg M-C teams of VGCPastes and Limitless, `munchstats-2026-10-03`: Rage Powder 525 teams, Flame Body 389, Psychic Fangs 270; plus Solar Beam, Clear Body, Hospitality and Overcoat, and the rows that only waited for a recorded battle). Fourteen rows: the moves Rage Powder, Sleep Powder, Stun Spore, Poison Powder, Psychic Fangs, Solar Beam, Matcha Gotcha, Giga Drain, Energy Ball and Play Rough, and the abilities Flame Body, Clear Body, Hospitality and Overcoat. No new public value (see View), no new state.
   - **The pin** (the Champions mod overrides none of the entries: `data/mods/champions/moves.ts` and `abilities.ts` have none of them but Mega Sol's `isNonstandard`). Moves (`data/moves.ts`): Rage Powder :14598-14632 (priority 2, flag `powder`, `volatileStatus: 'ragepowder'`, `onTry` `activePerHalf > 1`, a one-turn condition whose `onFoeRedirectTarget` at priority 1 returns the user when `source.runStatusImmunity('powder')` fails to stop it and `validTarget` holds); Psychic Fangs :14060-14078 (`onTryHit` removes Reflect, Light Screen and Aurora Veil of the target's side, in that order); Solar Beam :17224-17259 (`onTryMove`: `-prepare`, in sun `[still]` and `-anim` and the attack goes on, else `twoturnmove`; `onBasePower` x0.5 in rain, sand and snow); Sleep Powder :16851, Stun Spore :18274, Poison Powder :13512 (data, flag `powder`); Matcha Gotcha :11027, Giga Drain :6559, Energy Ball :4840, Play Rough :13420 (data). Abilities (`data/abilities.ts`): Flame Body :1316-1328, Clear Body :523-542, Hospitality :1874-1885, Overcoat :3108-3123. Engine: `hitStepTryImmunity` (`sim/battle-actions.ts:666-689`: the natural powder immunity first, then the Prankster test), spreadMoveHit's singleEvent `TryHit` (:1044, inside the hit loop after the accuracy check), `Battle.resolvePriority` (`sim/battle.ts:951-992`: an ability's handler sorts before an item's at one speed), `fieldEvent('SwitchIn')` and `getCallback` (:482-, :1020-1030: an ability's `onStart` is its SwitchIn handler at its `onSwitchInPriority`). The type chart's `powder: 3` is Grass alone. The generator reads the whole text of every callback and condition (`RAGE_POWDER_ONTRY`, `RAGE_POWDER_CONDITION`, `PSYCHIC_FANGS_ONTRYHIT`, `SOLAR_BEAM_CALLBACKS`, `G30_ABILITY_FACTS`: whitespace aside, with the Champions mod not overriding them) and the Grass-only powder immunity (`POWDER_IMMUNE_TYPES`), and fails on any other text.
   - **Tables.** Three handler ids: `RAGE_POWDER` 33, `PSYCHIC_FANGS` 34 and `SOLAR_BEAM` 35 (after G28's four, which follow G20's `SPIKY_SHIELD` 28), so UNMODELED is 36: the pool tables, their hash and the POOL fingerprints change (CLOSURE and TEAM_C do not). The powder flag is bit 16 of the second flags byte (`DFI_MOVE_FLAG2_POWDER`; the public static flag `DUOFORGE_MOVE_STATIC_FLAG_POWDER` of decision 0020 has no engine reader by rule, so the engine reads the byte). Flame Body, Clear Body, Hospitality and Overcoat are `ENGINE_ROWS` (handler NONE, run by id).
   - **Rage Powder needs no new state.** It shares the position's `DFI_VOL_FOLLOW_ME` bit with Follow Me, and the last move of the occupant (the tail's `last_move`, `dfi_last_move_id`) tells them apart: the bit is set by the move that its occupant is using, so that move is its `last_move`, and nothing but the occupant's next move changes it, which is after the residual has cleared the bit; a switch clears both; under a non-POOL kind `last_move` is always 0, so the bit is Follow Me's there. Follow Me's meaning is unchanged. The redirect loop of `dfi_run_move` skips a Rage Powder holder when the attacker is immune to powder (a Grass type, or Overcoat; Safety Goggles are not in the pool) and goes on to the next handler or the aimed target, with no line. A Follow Me and a Rage Powder that both apply on one side need the two handlers' order and are `E_UNSUPPORTED`, as two Follow Me holders were.
   - **View.** Rage Powder is the extension's `DUOFORGE_POSITION_EXT_RAGE_POWDER` (decision 0018 section 3.4, supported bit 39, public, a PIVOT boundary only: the residual ends it), filled from the same derivation; the basic view's `DUOFORGE_POSITION_FLAG_FOLLOW_ME` is not set for a Rage Powder user (it would say that every foe move is drawn). Both values were declared by decision 0018, so nothing is added to the header; the manifest sets bit 39. The Flame Body draw is the internal site `DFI_SITE_FLAME_BODY` 18 (17 is CURSED_BODY of G27), `DFI_SITE_COUNT` 19; the ps_trace harness names it `flamebody:DamagingHit` and the converter `FLAME_BODY`.
   - **Powder immunity and Overcoat** (`turn.c`). `hitStepTryImmunity`: a move with the powder flag stops at a Grass type of another Pokemon with a plain `-immune`, before the Prankster test; Overcoat's `onTryHit` (priority 1, the TryHit step with Good as Gold, `-immune ... [from] ability: Overcoat`) stops it for any other target; its `onImmunity` makes the holder immune to Sandstorm damage (`dfi_sand_immune`, and the sand check of `duoforge.state.pool_weather` no longer lists it). Overcoat is breakable; Mold Breaker is not marked.
   - **Psychic Fangs.** The singleEvent `TryHit` inside the hit loop: after the accuracy check (a miss or a Protect keeps the screens), before the damage of the hit, so that the hit is already without them; one `-sideend` line per screen that was up, in the order Reflect, Light Screen, Aurora Veil.
   - **Solar Beam** is the two-turn lock of Electro Shot (`charge_turns`, `locked_move`, `locked_target`; the converter's `solarbeam` volatile is `electroshot`'s: the lock is the state's `locked`, twoturnmove stays to the residual) without the Special Attack boost, with sun in place of rain, and x0.5 (a 2048 modifier at priority 0, after Knock Off's) in rain, sand and snow. The pin's `effectiveWeather` asks Utility Umbrella and Mega Sol and `ChargeMove` is Power Herb's event: none is marked, `duoforge.state.pool_g30` checks it.
   - **Flame Body, Clear Body, Hospitality.** Flame Body: the target's own DamagingHit handler, after Rough Skin and Rocky Helmet, before the attacker's Poison Touch; a contact move draws `randomChance(3, 10)` (`random(10) < 3`, one draw per target, also for a holder that the hit knocked out, as Rough Skin) and burns the attacker (`-status ... brn [from] ability: Flame Body [of] holder`; a Fire type, a status, a Flower Veil and Thermal Exchange stop it as for any status). Clear Body: Flower Veil's place in `dfi_boost` (after Contrary and the cap, a drop that another Pokemon causes is deleted; `-fail|holder|unboost|[from] ability: Clear Body|[of] holder` unless the effect is a move's secondary, which is a FAIL event with the ability as its cause and the holder as `other`, as Inner Focus's (step G22): no new event; the holder's own drops, Draco Meteor, pass; Parting Shot does not switch its user out); a Grass type with Clear Body or Trace would need an order with Flower Veil's handler and the pool has none (checked). Hospitality: a SwitchIn handler of priority -2 (after the entry abilities and the seeds, with White Herb's, the ability of one Pokemon before its own herb): the adjacent ally is healed by a quarter of its base maximum HP (`[-heal] ally [from] ability: Hospitality [of] holder`), nothing at full HP or under Heal Block; at once when Trace copies it.
   - **Not in this step.** Stomping Tantrum (309 teams) needs a last-move-failed flag: the position's flags byte is full and the tail has no field for it; a new revision is planned with HauptSession (rev 4) and the move stays unmarked. Cotton Spore (primary boosts on foes) and Magic Powder (a type change) stay unmodelled. Safety Goggles, Utility Umbrella and Power Herb are not in the pool; Mega Sol and Stomping Tantrum are unmarked, and `duoforge.state.pool_g30` says so.
   - **Evidence.** Fifteen recorded battles under the POOL kind (`g30_*`, genders stated, seeds picked by predicates): `g30_rage_powder_a` (Garchomp's single-target move is drawn, Rillaboom's, a Grass type, is not), `_pivot` (the redirected U-turn's switch request inside the turn: the RAGE_POWDER bit at that boundary only), `g30_overcoat_rage` (Overcoat is not drawn; its sand immunity), `g30_overcoat_powder`, `g30_powder_a` and `_b` (paralysis, poison and sleep from the powders, the Grass, Electric, Steel and Dark immunities, the Prankster fail, a status that fails on a statused target), `g30_psychic_fangs` (a Protect that stops it, three screens, two screens), `g30_solar_beam_sun_rain` (sun skips the charge, rain replaces the sun: charge, lock, half power) and `_sand_snow` (half power in each, a Protect on the locked turn), `g30_flame_body` and `_ko` (burns, a Fire attacker, the holder knocked out), `g30_clear_body` (the entry Intimidates, Parting Shot, Snarl, Spirit Break, Draco Meteor), `g30_hospitality` (a hurt ally healed, an ally at full HP) and `_trace` (Trace copies it), `g30_matcha_drain_rows` (Matcha Gotcha on both foes with the burn, Giga Drain, Energy Ball, Play Rough). `duoforge.state.pool_g30` replays them and reads what the reference does not show (the extension and the basic flag of every position after every step) and the facts above; the converter handles every new line (`-fail ... unboost`, `-singleturn ... Rage Powder`) and the draw. **Mutation checks.** Forty edits of `turn.c` and `observation.c`, each built and run against `duoforge.state.pool_g30` and the pool conformance tests: no natural powder immunity, no Overcoat TryHit, its line without the ability, no Overcoat sand immunity, a powder move that ignores a Grass type, Rage Powder redirecting a powder-immune attacker (Grass or Overcoat, each alone), no volatile, Follow Me's redirect inverted, the view showing Follow Me's flag or no RAGE_POWDER bit, Psychic Fangs breaking nothing, leaving Aurora Veil, the wrong order, no `-sideend`, Solar Beam charging in sun, skipping in rain, boosting Special Attack, full power in rain, sand and snow, halved in sun, quarter power, Electro Shot skipping in sun, Flame Body's threshold (3 in 10 against 4), not for a holder that is down, not for a standing holder, without contact, burning the holder, Clear Body showing for a secondary, blocking the holder's own drop, blocking nothing, without its line, Hospitality healing an eighth, never at the entry, twice after a Trace copy, not through Trace, healing the foe side: each fails a test, but one: the powder immunity checked for the user too is an equivalent mutant (Rage Powder, the only powder move that targets its user, returns before the hit loop). Two of them (the threshold and the missing contact test) needed a second look: the first recording had no draw of exactly 3 that a burn could follow and no non-contact hit on a Flame Body holder, so `g30_flame_body` got a seed with both and Incineroar's Snarl. **Campaign.** One mirror campaign over `tests/reference/teams/team_g30.txt` (Sinistcha, Venusaur, Metagross, Volcarona, Reuniclus, Whimsicott: every new row but Matcha Gotcha's secondaries in play; all items distinct, Item Clause) under the machine lock: `diff_driver.py random --battles 1500 --seed 3030 --pairings GG --workers 4 --domain-rate 0.1`, 1500 of 1500 PASS, 6246 requests sampled for the domain check and all agreed (a first attempt with repeated items was refused at the setup by Item Clause, 1500 `create` refusals, and is not evidence).
5v. **G32, small rules** (fifteen rows that need no new state: eleven moves, three abilities and an item, chosen from the "no state, batchable now" list of `docs/research/expansion/tail-rev4-proposal.md` by blocked teams). Marked: **Eruption** and **Water Spout** (`data/moves.ts:4888-4905`, `:20661-20678`), **Life Dew** (`:10286-10298`), **Body Press** (`:1572-1584`), **Foul Play** (`:6144-6157`), **Psyshock** (`:14264-14277`), **Rain Dance** (`:14679-14693`), **Sunny Day** (`:18416-18430`), **Volt Switch** (`:20432-20445`), **Clanging Scales** (`:2480-2497`), **Freeze-Dry** (`:6158-6177`, Champions mod `:415-418`), the abilities **Soundproof** (`data/abilities.ts:4436-4452`), **Unnerve** (`:5258-5275`) and **Speed Boost** (`:4453-4465`), and the item **Eject Button** (`data/items.ts:1680-1704`, Champions mod `data/mods/champions/items.ts:266-281`). **No public value, no new event, no view change, no tail change.** Tables: eight handlers `HP_POWER` (Eruption, Water Spout), `BODY_PRESS`, `FOUL_PLAY`, `PSYSHOCK`, `RAIN_DANCE`, `SUNNY_DAY`, `FREEZE_DRY`, `CLANGING_SCALES` (ids 36 to 43 after G30's Rage Powder, Psychic Fangs and Solar Beam at 33 to 35, UNMODELED 44; `G32_HANDLERS`), the target class `allies` (14) becomes an engine class (`ENGINE_TARGETS`: Life Dew, with the heal column 1/4), Volt Switch is a pivot with a switch flag of its own (`ENGINE_PIVOT_MOVES`, `DFI_SWITCH_VOLT_SWITCH` = 6, an internal state value: `switch_flag_max` of the POOL kinds, the pivot table and the Python state model; no byte of the layout), and Soundproof, Unnerve, Speed Boost and Eject Button are `ENGINE_ROWS`. The generator reads the texts the engine hard-codes (`G32_FACTS`, `G32_WEATHER_FACTS`, `G32_ENTRY_FACTS`; the Champions Freeze-Dry's `secondary: undefined` removes the freeze) and `tools/datagen/pool_families.js` calls the pinned callbacks (`checkG32`).
   - **Eruption, Water Spout**: power `floor(150 x HP / maximum HP)`, at least 1 (the callback returns a fraction and `getDamage` clamps it, `sim/battle-actions.ts`). **Body Press** attacks with the user's Defense, **Foul Play** with the target's Attack and its stages, **Psyshock** hits the Defense stat (`getDamage`, `:1671-1676`): the category stays the move's, so the user's ModifyAtk and ModifySpA, the weather's ModifyDef and ModifySpD of the stat that is hit, the crit rule and burn read it as before. **Freeze-Dry**: the Effectiveness event of the move answers 1 for the Water type (super effective whatever the chart says; Water at a dual type still adds its half). **Rain Dance**, **Sunny Day**: the move of Sandstorm and Snowscape for their weather (the same weather fails; 5 turns, the rock items are not marked). **Clanging Scales**: `selfBoost` (`sim/battle-actions.ts:520`), the user's Defense -1 after the hit loop when the move hit something, before Life Orb, nothing shown at -6. **Volt Switch**: U-turn's rule with its own flag (the switch line says `[from] Volt Switch`).
   - **Life Dew** (`allies`): `getMoveTargets` takes the standing members of the user's side in slot order, `runMove` aims the move at its user (`:419`), moveHit heals each target at its own full-HP check (`:1201-1222`: `-fail|target|heal` for a full one, `-heal` plain for the others) and the move line's `[spread]` list is the move's `hitTargets` after the hit steps: both slots when the heal did something for at least one target, an empty list when it did nothing. An ally under Heal Block is refused (`E_UNSUPPORTED`). The draw `RANDOM_TARGET execute:allies` (`getRandomTarget` for a class that is then overwritten) decides nothing and is dropped by the converter, as the spread classes' are.
   - **Soundproof**: `onTryHit`, in the TryHit stage with Flash Fire and Lightning Rod: a sound move aimed at the holder by another Pokemon is `-immune|holder|[from] ability: Soundproof`. **Unnerve**: `onFoeTryEatItem` returns false while a foe's holder stands, so a berry of the other side is not eaten (the resist berry neither weakens the hit nor shows a line; Sitrus Berry waits for the next Update); its `onStart` announces `-ability|holder|Unnerve` with `onSwitchInPriority` 1, so before the other entry abilities whatever the Speed order: `runSwitch` speedSorts every active Pokemon first (`:177-185`, the tie shuffle) and `resolvePriority` turns that order into a fractional speed (`sim/battle.ts:1008-1013`), so the priority only moves Unnerve ahead, and the tie draw that the engine and the converter already make for two entry bearers is unchanged. **Speed Boost**: `onResidual` at order 28, sub-order 2 (an ability handler, listed before the item's): +1 Speed, `-ability|holder|Speed Boost|boost` first, once the Pokemon has been active for a turn (`activeTurns` is the state's `NEWLY_SWITCHED` flag, which ends with the turn), nothing at +6. The handler is one more entry of the sorted residual list (15 per position; a "late callback" group with White Herb's after the side conditions' ends).
   - **Eject Button** (the Champions text, priority 2): after a damaging move of another Pokemon (an ally's too) hit a standing holder that has a reserve, and no active Pokemon has `switchFlag === true` (an Emergency Exit that is already set; the pivot moves' flags are strings), the holder sets its flag and uses the item (`-enditem`), and switches at the pivot request; it runs before the frozen cure (priority 0) and before the targets' Emergency Exit. The reference sorts AfterMoveSecondary handlers with `speedSort`, not left to right (`sim/battle.ts:788-796` lists only Invulnerability, TryHit, DamagingHit and EntryHazard): of two holders hit by one move the faster ejects (the other finds the flag set); **two holders at exactly one Speed are refused** (`E_UNSUPPORTED`: the shuffle has no converter context).
   - **Remaining refusals**: two Eject Button holders at one Speed in one move, and an ally under Heal Block that Life Dew would heal. Refusals met in the campaign: none.
   - **Evidence**: ten recorded POOL battles (genders stated, seeds by predicate): `g32_hp_power` (both moves at full HP and after Dragon Claw and Scald took HP), `g32_rain_sun` (Rain Dance, the same weather failing, Sunny Day replacing it, Water Spout under each, the upkeep lines), `g32_life_dew` (the user and its ally healed in slot order, then a full ally: `-fail ... heal`, the `[spread]` lists), `g32_override_attack` (Body Press, Foul Play before and after the target's Swords Dance), `g32_psyshock_freeze_dry` (Psyshock; Freeze-Dry super effective at Milotic), `g32_eject_button`, `g32_volt_switch`, `g32_clanging_soundproof` (Bastiodon's Soundproof and Kommo-o's; the Defense drop), `g32_speed_boost` (Blaziken each turn, Sharpedo switched in without a boost in its first turn), `g32_unnerve` (the announcement; a Sitrus Berry and a Charti Berry stay). 208 pool battles and 1132 stops (198 and 1072 before: six per new battle, counted from the `tiebreak_ref.h` diff), 106 marked moves (95 before), the generator tests (`SmallRules`), `pool_families` (`checkG32`). Mutation checks (21, each one edit of src/combat/turn.c, under the machine lock): 20 make conformance_pool_data red (Eruption ignoring the HP, Body Press with Attack, Foul Play with the user's Attack or stages, Psyshock at Special Defense, Freeze-Dry resisted or added to the chart, Rain Dance setting the sun, Life Dew healing only its user or showing no fail line, Soundproof unread, Eject Button never ejecting, Clanging Scales lowering nothing, Unnerve not reaching a resist berry or a Sitrus Berry, working on its own side or not announced first, Speed Boost in the entry turn, without its -ability line or sorted with the early callbacks; H16 and H20 were first green or unbuildable and the g32_unnerve battle was changed so that a Sitrus Berry holder falls to half HP). One survives: Eject Button ignoring a switch flag that is already true (an Emergency Exit set earlier in the same move, which needs a Rocky Helmet holder at Emergency Exit's threshold in front of an Eject Button holder: no recorded battle has it). Campaign: two new teams (J: Typhlosion, Blastoise, Milotic with Eject Button, Raichu, Alakazam, Baxcalibur; K: Kommo-o, Blaziken, Tyranitar, Umbreon, Aggron, Charizard) against each other, themselves and teams A and B, seed 3201, 240 battles under the machine lock: 240 PASS, no UNSUPPORTED (no refusal of the step was met), 917 sampled requests all agreed.
5w. **G34, small rules and data rows** (a batch of E's audit of the rev 4 blockers, `tail-rev4-proposal.md` section 6: rows that need a small rule or none). Fifteen rows: the moves Steel Roller, Clangorous Soul, Brick Break, Fiery Dance, Psycho Cut, Iron Defense and Electroweb, the abilities Compound Eyes, Iron Fist, Sharpness, Solid Rock, Technician, Multiscale and Gale Wings, and the item Wide Lens. No new public value, no new state, no view change.
   - **The pin** (the Champions mod overrides none of them but Clangorous Soul, whose override has `accuracy: true`; the generator reads the override, `G34_FACTS`, `G34_ABILITY_FACTS` and `G34_ITEM_FACTS` check the pinned text of every callback it relies on). Moves (`data/moves.ts`): Steel Roller :17893-17913 (`onTry` fails without a terrain, `onHit` clears it), Clangorous Soul, Brick Break :1822-1840 (`onTryHit` breaks the three screens, the text of Psychic Fangs), Fiery Dance, Psycho Cut, Iron Defense, Electroweb (data). Abilities (`data/abilities.ts`): Compound Eyes :666-677 (ModifyAccuracy, priority -1, 5325), Iron Fist :2236-2248 (BasePower 23, 4915, punch flag), Sharpness :4174-4186 (BasePower 19, 6144, slicing flag), Solid Rock :4414-4425 (SourceModifyDamage, x0.75 when typeMod > 0), Technician :4916-4930 (BasePower 30, 6144 for 60 or less), Multiscale :2760-2771 (SourceModifyDamage, x0.5 at full HP), Gale Wings :1588-1596 (+1 priority for a Flying move at full HP). Wide Lens `data/items.ts:7713-7727` (ModifyAccuracy, priority -2, 4505).
   - **Tables.** Three handler ids: `STEEL_ROLLER` 44, `CLANGOROUS_SOUL` 45 and `BRICK_BREAK` 46, after the eight of G32 (UNMODELED moves to 47); the seven abilities and Wide Lens are `ENGINE_ROWS`; the second flags byte gets the internal bits `PUNCH` (32) and `SLICING` (64) (`FLAGS2_BITS`, `DFI_MOVE_FLAG2_PUNCH` and `_SLICING`). **The engine reads only these internal bits**, never the static dex features of decision 0020 (the cmake check `static_columns_unread.cmake` still holds); `tests/test_pool_g34.c` pins that the two internal bits equal the set of the public static column. Fiery Dance's 50 percent Special Attack boost is the secondary kind `SECONDARY_SELF_BOOST` of G28 (Ancient Power's), as data. The POOL table hash and the fingerprints change as for every pool step.
   - **Base power, accuracy, priority** (`turn.c`). Technician is judged at the pin's point: the first handler (priority 30) on the move's own power after its callback, with the chain still 4096; then the -ate abilities and Iron Fist (23), Fairy Aura (20), Sharpness (19), the type booster (15), Helping Hand (10), the terrains (6). Compound Eyes and Wide Lens chain into one accuracy modifier before the stage calculation, and a move that never misses is untouched. Gale Wings is in `dfi_move_priority`: +1 for a Flying move of a holder at full HP, judged on the type the move has before ModifyType.
   - **Steel Roller** fails without a terrain (`-fail`, [still]); after the damage of the hit the terrain ends (`-fieldend`, then the TerrainChange event of the seeds), once per move, before the self switch and the secondaries. The engine has Grassy and Psychic Terrain today, so those are what it ends; **G25 (Electric and Misty Terrain, pending the owner) extends it** to those, in `dfi_clear_terrain`.
   - **Clangorous Soul** fails at one third of the HP or less (`hp * 100 <= hp_max * 33`) and with a maximum HP of 1, otherwise raises the five stats and costs the user a third of its maximum HP, rounded down, at least 1 (a plain damage event after the boost), then the status hit end. **Brick Break** shares Psychic Fangs' block: after the accuracy check, so a miss or a Protect keeps the screens.
   - **Solid Rock and Multiscale join the ModifyDamage chain, and the commutation proof is general.** One hit can now have Life Orb or Expert Belt, a resist berry, a screen, Glaive Rush's volatile, and Solid Rock or Multiscale. The handlers have no order and priority 0, so the pin's `runEvent` sorts them by their holders' speed, then subOrder (a condition 2, an ability 7, an item 8; a side condition has no speed and is last), and shuffles only a complete tie; the engine does not compute that order. It instead needs every order to chain to one value: `dfi_mods_commute` (`src/combat/damage_chain.h`, header only, Heap's algorithm over at most six values, no allocation) and the engine returns E_UNSUPPORTED for a hit whose modifiers do not commute, no silent fallback. Of the 71 possible combinations 53 commute and 18 do not (listed in the next bullet); this replaces G19's rule "three or more with Glaive Rush", which let Expert Belt with a berry and Glaive Rush through (a main gap since G28, found while checking Solid Rock). The refusal is conservative: the order is determined by the state. **Follow-up** (not in G34): compute the order exactly (the holder speeds, the subOrder values, a tie only when the result depends on it) and refuse only a tie that changes the result, verified by a differential campaign over Glaive Rush, Expert Belt, Life Orb, berry, screen, Solid Rock and Multiscale teams; the measured rate of the refusal below decides its priority. The converter (`trace_to_c.py`, `modifiers_commute`) holds the same values for its drop rule of a tied ModifyDamage group (one handler of each kind, at most one berry; a group that is not one hit stays refused as before).
   - **Evidence.** Twelve recorded battles under the POOL kind (`g34_*`): `steel_roller`, `brick_break`, `clangorous_soul`, `gale_wings`, `accuracy` (seed `0x3480`: the accuracy draws of Sleep Powder with Compound Eyes and Fire Blast with Wide Lens sit between the plain and the modified threshold), `base_power`, `damage_chain` (Solid Rock with a Rindo Berry against a Life Orb; Multiscale at full HP and not at the second hit), `data_rows`, and four that the first mutation run asked for: `gale_priority` (Will-O-Wisp at full HP gets no priority; Brave Bird at full HP is +1 and Raichu's faster Quick Attack still goes first), `visible_power` (Psycho Cut, Night Slash, Close Combat and Mach Punch on targets that survive, so Sharpness and Iron Fist show in the damage), `wide_lens_miss` (seed `0x3504`: a draw of 97 misses with the lens, whose accuracy is 93) and `steel_roller_seeds` (a Steel Roller into a Protect leaves the terrain; the next one ends it, and two Raichu with a Psychic Seed of equal Speed draw the TerrainChange tie). `duoforge.state.pool_g34` pins the marks, ids and flag bits and compares `dfi_mods_commute` with a brute-force walk of all 71 subsets. **Mutation checks.** 45 edits of `turn.c` and `damage_chain.h`, each built and run against `duoforge.state.pool_g34` and the pool conformance tests: Gale Wings without the full HP condition, for any move, with +2, without the ability, above half HP; Technician at 80, 1.2 and gone; Iron Fist for any move, with 1.5, on the slicing flag; Sharpness for any move, with 1.2, on the punch flag; Solid Rock for every hit, 0.8, only for a double super effective; Multiscale at any HP, never, 0.75; the refusal gone, only from four modifiers, inverted; the commutation test with a tolerance of one and with the first order only; Compound Eyes 1.1 and gone; Wide Lens 1.3 and gone; Steel Roller without its fail, keeping the terrain, clearing on a miss, without `-fieldend`, without TerrainChange, keeping the terrain turns, ending the wrong terrain; Clangorous Soul without the HP condition, costing a quarter, nothing or a half, without the boosts; Brick Break breaking nothing. 42 failed a test. The first run left eleven alive, which showed that the battles did not exercise them: Gale Wings for any move and with +2 (the Raichu that hit Talonflame took its full HP), Sharpness and Iron Fist wherever the target fainted, Wide Lens 1.3 (no draw between 93 and 99), Steel Roller on a miss and its TerrainChange tie (no Protect, no two seed holders), and the engine's refusal itself (only the helper was tested). The four new battles and the refusal test in `pool_g34` (g34_damage_chain with an Expert Belt in the place of the Life Orb: E_UNSUPPORTED from the step of the first Wood Hammer at the Rhyperior) kill them. Three edits cannot fail a test and are not reachable: Technician at `< 60` (no Technician holder has a marked move of exactly 60 power; the pin's `<= 60` is a checked text of the generator), the accuracy modifier on a move that never misses (the modified 0 is 0), and Clangorous Soul at exactly one third (it needs a maximum HP divisible by 100, and Kommo-o has 150 to 182). **Campaigns** under the machine lock, with a throwaway local build (not committed) that logs every firing of the new refusal: `diff_driver.py random --seed 3434 --battles 1500 --pairings DD,DE,ED,EE --workers 4 --domain-rate 0.1` over `tests/reference/teams/team_g34.txt` and `team_g34b.txt` (the fifteen rows in play, distinct items per team): 1500 of 1500 PASS, 26540 steps, 4863 domain requests agreed, **the refusal fired 0 times** (0 battles, 0 steps; no UNSUPPORTED of any kind). A registry-mix run of the realistic rate, `--seed 3435 --battles 2000` over 200 random pairs of the registry teams of `data/teams` (649 ids besides A, B and C): 2000 of 2000 PASS, 30609 steps, 5414 domain requests agreed, **the refusal fired 0 times** (0 of 2000 battles, 0 of 30609 steps). The 18 refused combinations need three or more of the modifiers at once (Expert Belt or Life Orb, a matching resist berry, a screen, the Glaive Rush volatile, Solid Rock or Multiscale); no battle of these runs had one that fails to commute, so the conservative refusal cost the learner nothing that was measured, and the exact-order follow-up is not urgent.
5x. **G36, Toxic and the badly poisoned status** (the tail's `toxic_stage` per roster member, rev 2 as it is, and the ailment `DUOFORGE_AILMENT_TOX` = 6 that decision 0018 and the header already have: **no new public value**, no new event or cause, no layout change). Toxic and Poison Fang become modelled rows (the generator's pool status table gains `tox` = 6; under CLOSURE and TEAM_C the tables are byte for byte the same).
   - **The pin.** Toxic (`data/moves.ts:19733-19748`): Poison, Status, PP 10, accuracy 90, flags protect, reflectable, mirror, `status: 'tox'`, no Champions override. A Poison-type user never misses with it: the accuracy step and the invulnerability step test `move.id === 'toxic' && pokemon.hasType('Poison')` for gen 8 and later (`sim/battle-actions.ts:627` and `:731`), so no accuracy draw. Poison Fang (`:13463-13480`): 50 percent `status: 'tox'` secondary, contact and bite. The condition `tox` (`data/conditions.ts:138-161`): `onStart` sets the stage to 0 and prints `-status|X|tox` (with `[from] item: Toxic Orb`, or `[from] ability: NAME [of] source`, which no pool battle has); `onSwitchIn` sets the stage to 0 again (the status stays on the bench; Gen 9 has no switch-out cure); `onResidualOrder: 9` (psn's is 9 too, `:123-137`; no sub-order, so the two sort in one group with burn's 10 after them): the stage goes up by one to at most 15 first, then `damage(clampIntRange(baseMaxhp / 16, 1) * stage)`; the line is `-damage|X|HP tox|[from] psn` (the source it names is psn). A status that is set already refuses another (`-fail|X|tox` for the same one, `-fail` for another), and Poison and Steel types are immune to tox through the `psn` key of the type chart (`sim/pokemon.ts:1706-1722`: `runStatusImmunity(status.id === 'tox' ? 'psn' : status.id)`, `-immune` for a move's own status), unless the source has Corrosion.
   - **State, view, tables.** The member's status byte may be 6 under the POOL kinds (their bound was 5; the other kinds are unchanged), with no status counter; the stage lives in the tail (0 at the start and after a switch-in or a faint, the invariant: a stage needs the status tox and a member on the field, which is now reachable). The view shows the ailment Tox in the old observation's status field (the stage is hidden, as the game hides it) and sets the supported bit 10 (`AILMENT_TOX`). UNMODELED moves go from 262 to 260 (Toxic and Poison Fang were modelled except for `status tox`).
   - **Rules of the turn.** The status goes through the same `dfi_try_status` as the others (immunities, Flower Veil, the fail lines); the residual entry of poison also takes tox (order 9, sub-order 0, a callback: two of them of equal Speed shuffle), and its handler raises the stage and deals the damage above.
   - **Not in this step (and why).** Toxic Spikes (`data/moves.ts:19749-19788`) and Toxic Debris (`data/abilities.ts:5104-5117`, Glimmora) need a side condition with layers that a switch-in reads: a new `SIDE` value and the hazard state, a step of their own; both stay unmodelled. Not in the pool: Toxic Orb (`data/mods/champions/items.ts:970`, "Past"), Malignant Chain ("Past"), Flame Orb, Toxic Chain (no pool forme has it; `data/abilities.ts:5089-5103`). In the pool but unmarked, so no accepted battle has them: Corrosion (Salazzle, Glimmora: it would let Poison and Steel types be poisoned, `data/abilities.ts` corrosion and `sim/pokemon.ts:1708-1710`), Poison Heal (Gliscor), Merciless (Toxapex), Immunity (Snorlax), Pastel Veil, Magic Guard, Synchronize, Guts and Quick Feet, and the berries that cure poison (Pecha, Lum), Venoshock and Hex. A step that marks one of them reads these lines for tox. The 15-stage cap is in the code and the pin, and no recorded battle reaches it (a tox stage of 15 needs 15 residuals without a faint).
   - **Evidence.** Seven recorded battles under the POOL kind (`g36_*`, seeds by predicates; 227 pool battles, 1237 tiebreak stops, 46 view and tail rows in `duoforge.state.pool_g36`): `g36_toxic_a` (a Poison-type user without an accuracy draw, a Dark-type user with one, the stages 1 to 7 of the residual damage, a switch-out and back that restarts the stage while the status stays, a second Toxic that fails with `-fail|X|tox`), `g36_toxic_fail` (the immunities of a Steel and a Poison type), `g36_poison_fang_a` and `_b` (the 50 percent secondary, both outcomes), `g36_toxic_mirror_a` and `_b` (two Umbreon of equal Speed badly poisoning each other: the damage lines in both orders of the shuffle) and `g36_toxic_burn` (tox before burn in the residual, whatever the Speed).
