Eight mutants of the rule (holder not exempt, Ghost not exempt, a fainted holder still trapping, the own side asked instead of the foes, only the foe's first position asked, trapped at every boundary, never trapped, only the first position trapped) are each caught by `duoforge.state.pool_g41`; two of them survived the first round (the fainted holder and the first-position probe), which is why `g41_shadow_tag_fainted` and `g41_shadow_tag_second` exist. Differential: 300 battles of two Mega Gengar teams (no Substitute) pass in 300 of 300 with 1446 judged requests (about one in ten with a trap in the request), plus 300 earlier battles with the unmapped request change (299 pass, one unsupported: a poison KO with a Tailwind tie, unrelated to the trap). **Known gap**: Gengar sets with Substitute stay blocked (Substitute is not marked).

5al. **G48, four moves on the fields that tail rev 4 already has (Rage Fist, Stone Axe, Ceaseless Edge, Population Bomb; Quick Guard dropped)**. No new public value, no event kind, no state field, no view change: `hits_taken` and the hazard fields of G37 are the state. The batch takes the rows that need nothing beyond those fields; the row that needs a public value is dropped.
   - **Dropped: Quick Guard** (data/moves.ts:14489-14520). Its block line `-activate|X|move: Quick Guard` needs a new BLOCKED detail (`DUOFORGE_BLOCK_QUICK_GUARD`), a public value that the lead has not approved; the side condition, the stall counter (Wide Guard's `addVolatile('stall')`) and the view bit `DUOFORGE_SIDE_GUARD_QUICK_GUARD` already exist, so the row waits for that value alone.
   - **Rage Fist** (data/moves.ts:14583-14596; the Champions mod only inherits it, data/mods/champions/moves.ts:793-796, and resets the counter in Pokemon#clearVolatile, data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:123-170). Power `min(350, 50 + 50 x timesAttacked)` of the user. The pin counts the damaging hits of a move on its target after the move (scripts.ts:565, sim/battle-actions.ts:994, `hit - 1`); the engine counts each one at the damage of that hit, under the POOL kinds only, saturating at 6 (which is already 350), in the hit loop of `dfi_run_move`. A switch-out and a faint clear the position (`dfi_tail_clear_occupant`, which is the pin's clearVolatile). Learner: Annihilape with Inner Focus or Defiant (both marked).
   - **Stone Axe and Ceaseless Edge** (data/moves.ts:18069-18095 and 2220-2246). `onAfterHit` runs for every damaged target, after DamagingHit and before the attacker's Emergency Exit, with no test of the user's HP (data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:411-414): `source.side.foeSidesWithConditions()` gets Stealth Rock (Stone Axe) or one Spikes layer (Ceaseless Edge) through the G37 add, which puts a layer or does nothing and prints nothing when the side is full. Both carry an empty `secondary: {}` (the Sheer Force placeholder): without Sheer Force the pin's `secondaries()` rolls `random(100)` for each hit target and always applies the empty secondary (sim/battle-actions.ts:1336-1349), so the engine draws one SECONDARY roll per hit target and does nothing else (found by the first recorded battle: 4 draws against 3). Sheer Force is unmarked, so a holder is refused. The `onAfterSubDamage` of both is for a Substitute, which is not marked. Learners: Kleavor (Swarm) and Samurott-Hisui (Torrent), both marked.
   - **Population Bomb** (data/moves.ts:13613-13626; no Champions entry). `multihit: 10, multiaccuracy: true`, accuracy 90, so the later-hit proof of G33 (`dfi_multiaccuracy_proven(90)`) covers it. The hit loop is the G33 one with the hit count 10 (`DFI_SPECIAL_MULTI_HIT_10`). Learners: Maushold and Maushold-Four with Friend Guard (marked by G35) or Technician; Cheek Pouch stays unmarked.
   - **Rows of the table**: handler ids `RAGE_FIST` (63), `STONE_AXE` (64), `CEASELESS_EDGE` (65) and `MULTI_HIT_10` (66) after Taunt and Yawn; UNMODELED moves to 67 (217 unmodelled moves, from 221). The generator checks the four whole texts (`G48_FACTS`) and the owned multihit and secondary texts. The POOL canonical hash, the header hashes, the POOL fingerprints (KP and KPD), the counts and the tiebreak counts are regenerated or recounted.
   - **Evidence**: eleven recorded POOL battles with genders stated (`g48_rage_fist_a`, `g48_rage_fist_switch`, `g48_stone_axe_rock`, `g48_stone_axe_miss`, `g48_ceaseless_edge_spikes`, `g48_population_bomb_fg` (four hits, a miss at the fifth) and `_a` to `_d` (`_b` hits all ten times, `_a` and `_d` miss at the second hit, `_c` at the first); `g48_population_bomb_technician`: a Technician user, four hits and a miss). `tests/test_pool_g48.c` (`duoforge.state.pool_g48`): the marks and the constants; the hits taken by each position and the hazard layers after every step (rows derived from the committed traces by a scratch helper that is not committed, one hit per public -damage line, reset on a switch or a faint); the bound (six valid, seven refused); the power by the hits in the state (20, 40, 120 and 141 HP of damage for 0, 1, 5 and 6 hits, the cap 350 included). `pool_tail`'s expected tail after a turn now counts the hits of the step from its DAMAGE events.
   - **Mutation checks**: 15 of 15 caught, on the final tree (one edit each in `turn.c` or `identity.c`; the driver restores the file and rebuilds). Killed by `duoforge.state.pool_g48` (G48) or `duoforge.reference.conformance_pool_data` (the battles), with the other test named: no hit counted (also the tail test); the hits saturate at 5 (G48 only: the power check); a hit counted on the user (G48 only); counted under the other kinds (the closure conformance and the tail test); the hits not cleared on a switch (four tests); the Rage Fist cap 300 (G48 only: the power check); Stone Axe on the own side, Stone Axe sets Spikes, Ceaseless Edge sets Stealth Rock (both tests); no after-hit hazard (both); no secondary roll (the battles: a tape of one roll too many or too few); Population Bomb with nine hits (the battles); Population Bomb without multiaccuracy (both); the Stone Axe hazard after a miss (both: the miss battle `g48_stone_axe_miss` was added for it; before that battle this one survived). The first run of M11 did not compile under `-Werror` (a constant condition); it was rerun with a condition that compiles, and it is killed. The cap at 350 itself has no mutant: six hits is the saturation, so 350 is also what 50 + 50 x 6 gives.
5am. **G43, Mega batch 3: ten Mega Stones, mark only** (Dragoninite, Glimmoranite, Blazikenite, Absolite Z, Raichunite X, Lopunnite, Alakazite, Meowsticite for both Meowstic formes, Scizorite and Galladite). Mark only on the Mega-by-stone model of 5p, as Mega batch 2 (5ag): no rule, no engine row, no handler, no state, no public value, no version bump. The ten stones were handler NONE rows already, so `pool_tables` (canonical sha256 `2515ab6a…`, `gen_closure.py --pool --check` ok) and the POOL fingerprint are unchanged; the change is the manifest, the tests and the battles.
   - **The pin.** Each stone is a `megaStone` item of `data/items.ts` (Absolite Z 46-57, Alakazite 215-226, Blazikenite 568-579, Dragoninite 1511-1522, Galladite 2374-2385, Glimmoranite 2508-2519, Lopunnite 3469-3480, Meowsticite 3927-3942, Raichunite X 5035-5046, Scizorite 5530-5541) and `inherit: true` in `data/mods/champions/items.ts` (14-17, 46-49, 110-113, 226-229, 366-369, 394-397, 538-541, 626-629, 762-765, 838-841). The Mega formes (`data/pokedex.ts`): Dragonite-Mega 3365-3379, Glimmora-Mega 18587-18600, Blaziken-Mega 5117-5131, Absol-Mega-Z 6722-6736, Raichu-Mega-X 837-851, Lopunny-Mega 7932-7945, Alakazam-Mega 1616-1630, Meowstic-M-Mega 12743-12759, Meowstic-F-Mega 12760-12776, Scizor-Mega 4400-4413, Gallade-Mega 8661-8675. The Champions mod has no entry for any of the abilities below.
   - **The support.** The Mega abilities were already marked: Multiscale, Adaptability, Speed Boost, Sharpness, Electric Surge, Scrappy, Trace, Technician and Inner Focus. The base abilities the recorded battles use are marked and legal for their base formes (`data/pokedex.ts`): Inner Focus (Dragonite, Alakazam), Toxic Debris (Glimmora), Speed Boost (Blaziken), Justified (Absol), Lightning Rod (Raichu), Limber (Lopunny), Prankster (Meowstic) and Competitive (Meowstic-F), Technician (Scizor), Sharpness (Gallade).
   - **What the Mega changes, and why it needs no rule.** The types of an evolved member come from its forme row (`dfi_forme_of`). Absol-Mega-Z is Dark/Ghost (base Dark) and Lopunny-Mega is Normal/Fighting (base Normal); the others keep their base types. The weights differ (Dragonite-Mega 290 kg against 210 kg); Low Kick and Grass Knot read the Mega forme's weight (`turn.c:1367-1368`), as they did for every earlier Mega. Trace (Alakazam-Mega, both Meowstic-Megas) copies a foe's ability after the Mega Evolution along the existing path (AC1, `dfi_trace`); the foes of these battles hold marked abilities only. Multiscale (full HP only), Inner Focus (no flinch) and the rest are the engine's existing rules.
   - **Evidence.** Eleven recorded POOL battles, `g43_<stone>` (`g43_meowsticite_m` and `g43_meowsticite_f`), every gendered Pokemon's gender stated in its paste, each holder Mega Evolving on turn 1: `g43_dragoninite` (Dragonite (M); the first Fake Out hits it at full HP right after the Mega, Multiscale's halving checked by the HP lines; the second one hits it below full HP); `g43_glimmoranite` (Glimmora (F); Sludge Bomb after the Mega, Adaptability's STAB); `g43_blazikenite` (Blaziken (M); Speed Boost raises its Speed at the end of the turn); `g43_absolitez` (Absol (M); the Mega-Z forme is Ghost, so the two Normal Fake Outs show `-immune`, and Night Slash is Sharpness's); `g43_raichunitex` (Raichu (F); `-fieldstart Electric Terrain [from] ability: Electric Surge`); `g43_lopunnite` (Lopunny (F); Quick Attack hits Gholdengo, a Ghost type, under Scrappy); `g43_alakazite` (Alakazam (F); Trace copies Raichu's Lightning Rod after the Mega); `g43_meowsticite_m` (Meowstic (M)) and `g43_meowsticite_f` (Meowstic-F (F)); Trace copies Incineroar's Intimidate after the Mega; `g43_scizorite` (Scizor (M); Bullet Punch under Technician, HP lines); `g43_galladite` (Gallade (M); Inner Focus, the two Fake Outs in turn 1 do not stop its Close Combat). Each spec runs four steps under `"data": "pool"`; the traces are `ps_trace.js` output and the converter's `conformance_pool.h` has 335 battles.
   - **Tests.** `tests/test_pool_g43.c` (`duoforge.state.pool_g43`): the marks and handler NONE of the ten stones; the eleven (base, stone, Mega) pairs with their Mega ability, base ability and support; the setup with each stone accepted, Mega capable, evolving once into its forme; the public API (`duoforge_data_mega_count` and `_at`) reports each pair as supported. `tests/test_pool_tables.c`: the stone predicate lists the ten ids, and Absolite Z is now marked. `tests/test_conformance.c`: 335 POOL battles. `tests/test_tiebreak.c`: 335 battles and 1929 stops (55 more). `trace_to_c.py --check`, `tiebreak_ref.js --check` and `ps_trace.js --check` pass; `test_trace_to_c.py`, `test_gen_closure.py`, `test_pool_rows.py`: 220 passed.
   - **Mutation.** One un-marking per stone (Meowsticite removes both Meowstic pairs): ten mutants, each rebuilt and run against `duoforge.state.pool_g43`, `reference.conformance_pool_data`, `reference.tiebreak_pool_data`, `data.pool_tables`, `state.mega_by_stone`, `state.pool_setup` and `data.api`. **10 of 10 caught**, no survivor.
   - **Campaign.** `tools/cloud/aws_fuzz/campaigns/g43-mega3` (teams D, E, F: the holders with Milotic, Staraptor, Gholdengo, Incineroar, Sneasler, Pelipper and Kingambit; fifteen pairings among them and with teams A and B; seed 9743001, the campaign.conf of one chunk of 300). Local run through `diff_driver.py random`: **PASS 300**, DIVERGENCE 0, UNSUPPORTED 0, ORACLE_GAP 0, REF_ERROR 0, CAP 0; domain check 893 of 893 requests agreed. The AWS launch is not part of this step.
   - **Not in this batch.** Lucarionite (the other Lucario stone) and Absolite (Magic Bounce) stay unmarked: they follow in later lane B batches.

5an. **G44, simple moves** (no public value, no new state, no event, no view change; the handler ids of the pool tables and the draw sites of the reference are unchanged). Handlers: **Thunder** (`DFI_SPECIAL_THUNDER`, `data/moves.ts:19438-19466`: `onModifyMove` on `target?.effectiveWeather()`, `move.accuracy = true` in rain, 50 under sun; the engine's Hurricane branch, `src/combat/turn.c` accuracy step), **Power Trip** (`DFI_SPECIAL_POWER_TRIP`, `data/moves.ts:13851-13859`: BasePower 20 plus 20 times `pokemon.positiveBoosts()`, the sum of the user's positive stages over the seven stats, `sim/pokemon.ts:1201-1208`; the stages are read from the attacker's position), **Ice Fang** (`DFI_SPECIAL_ICE_FANG`, `data/moves.ts:9347-9368`: two secondaries in order, a freeze roll then a flinch roll, each one `random(100) < 10` of `DFI_SITE_SECONDARY`, drawn per hit target even after a knock-out, as `secondaries()` does at `sim/battle-actions.ts:1336-1354`), and **Tri Attack** (`DFI_SPECIAL_TRI_ATTACK`, `data/moves.ts:19845-19864`: a secondary of chance 20, one SECONDARY roll per hit target, then `sample(['brn', 'par', 'frz'])` on `DFI_SITE_STATUS_PICK` (`random(3)`) and `trySetStatus` without a source move, as Dire Claw's pick does; the pick is drawn after every successful roll). **Data rows** (no handler, marked in the manifest only): Breaking Swipe (`data/moves.ts:1789-1806`, allAdjacentFoes, 100 percent Attack drop), Vacuum Wave (`:20282-20294`, priority +1, Special, 40), Stone Edge (`:18096-18109`, accuracy 80, critRatio 2), Aqua Cutter (`:439-452`, critRatio 2, slicing), Hammer Arm (`:8085-8102`, the user's Speed -1), Trop Kick (`:20057-20075`, Attack -1; the Champions mod sets BasePower 85, `data/mods/champions/moves.ts:1084-1087`), Meteor Mash (`:11763-11783`, 20 percent self Attack +1, the G28 self secondary). The generator maps the four handlers with their owned texts (`G44_FACTS`, `G2_OWNED_FIELDS['ICE_FANG']`, `G2_OWNED_SECONDARY['TRI_ATTACK']`); `checkG44` in `tools/datagen/pool_families.js` calls the pinned callbacks with stubs (the Power Trip values 20, 60, 80, 860; Thunder's accuracy by weather; the freeze and flinch order; the pick). **Not marked**: **Scale Shot** (`data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:427-470`: a hit count of 2 to 5 is `sample` over 20 values, a draw of its own; no draw site of the reference has it, and a new site is a header constant of `src/rng/draw.h`, so the row stays UNMODELED for the lead's decision; its self boosts are the G33 loop's own), and **Thunder through Fly**: Fly is not marked, so no battle has a Thunder that meets a semi-invulnerable target. **Evidence**: 19 recorded POOL battles (`g44_*`: Thunder under Drizzle and under Drought (50 percent: five misses of nine Thunders in one battle, one in the others), Power Trip after Bulk Up (two positive stages, BasePower 60), Ice Fang with freezes and one flinch (`g44_ice_fang_flinch_h`, the target has not moved yet), Tri Attack with burns, a paralysis and freezes, Breaking Swipe (both foes), Vacuum Wave, Stone Edge, Aqua Cutter, Hammer Arm, Trop Kick and Meteor Mash with its self boost); the foes protect with their move 2 so that the user lives and acts for the recorded turns. `tests/test_pool_g44.c` (`duoforge.state.pool_g44`) checks the marks, the handler ids, the pinned numbers of each row and the two unmarked cases. Tables regenerated (`gen_closure.py --pool`): 213 UNMODELED moves (217 before this step, after step G48 has taken its four out), the special id of UNMODELED 71 (the handlers of G44 are 67 to 70, after the four of G48), the POOL table hash `403ef55d32c8502b978197f6f775dd6edc2cc415edea29de88d3e15fb7f4ba0b` (the state model's `POOL_TABLE_HASH` had a stale value `fb0826fe...`, which this step replaces: the POOL fingerprints of `tests/test_pool_setup.c` are the model's now). **Mutation checks** (14 mutants of the four rules and the manifest, each rebuilt and run against `duoforge.state.pool_g44`, `duoforge.reference.conformance_pool_data` and `duoforge.data.pool_tables`; the final run): 13 caught, 1 equivalent. The caught ones: the Thunder rule removed (conformance), the sun accuracy 51 (conformance: the battle `g44_thunder_sun_roll50` has a single accuracy draw of 50 under sun, which misses), the Power Trip step of 25 and the target's stages (conformance), Power Trip counting five stats (white-box: the helper `dfi_power_trip_positive_stages` at accuracy and evasion stages), the Ice Fang flinch removed, the freeze roll at 9 (conformance: `g44_ice_fang_freeze9`, the freeze lands on the roll 9), the flinch reusing the freeze roll, the Tri Attack pick order and the missing pick (conformance), the Tri Attack chance 19 (white-box: `dfi_tri_attack_chance_hit` at 19 and 20), Vacuum Wave unmarked and Scale Shot marked (the manifest test and the white-box marks). **The equivalent mutant**: the Tri Attack status with the move as its source (`move_id` instead of `DFI_NO_SOURCE_MOVE`) changes nothing: inside `dfi_try_status` the source move is read only by the sleep branch (`src/combat/turn.c`, the `DFI_STATUS_SLP` test), and Tri Attack's pick is brn, par or frz, so no output can differ. Threshold boundaries that no seed produced: none left, the Tri Attack chance is a helper tested at 0, 19, 20 and 99. **Differential campaign** (`diff_driver.py random`, seed 44, 300 battles, pairings DA, AD, DE, ED, EA, AE with the two teams of this step): PASS 300 of 300, 941 domain samples accepted, 0 dropped; the traces show 124 Power Trips, 156 Thunders, 294 Ice Fangs, 275 Tri Attacks, 316 Breaking Swipes, 165 Vacuum Waves, 196 Stone Edges, 176 Aqua Cutters, 247 Hammer Arms, 306 Trop Kicks, 139 Meteor Mashes, 21 freezes, 20 burns and 70 paralyses (Scale Shot: 0, unmarked). **Gaps closed in the review of this PR**: Aqua Cutter's critical hit is recorded (`g44_aqua_cutter_crit`, seed 0x5001: the CRIT draw of a critRatio 2 move hits). Vacuum Wave's priority against faster foes is recorded (`g44_vacuum_wave_faster_foe`: Scizor, speed 65, moves before Gholdengo, Milotic and Primarina, which attack with priority 0). Scale Shot stays unmarked and is left for the next batch (the lead approves a new draw site in `src/rng/draw.h` there). **The guard** (`tests/test_pool_g44.c`, `check_guard_unmarked`): Ice Fang and Tri Attack bypass the generic secondaries path, so they skip the pin's `ModifySecondaries` (`sim/battle-actions.ts:1340-1341`), which Shield Dust, Covert Cloak and Serene Grace change. The test asserts that Shield Dust (a pool row, `DFI_ABILITY_SHIELDDUST`) is unmarked, and that no row named `serenegrace` or `covertcloak` is marked; neither of the two is a row of the pool today. A later step that adds one of them must honour `ModifySecondaries` on these two paths first, or the test fails. **The boundary seeds**: sun accuracy 50 (`g44_thunder_sun_roll50`, seed 0x519b, sun active, the single accuracy draw is 50 and misses), the freeze roll 9 (`g44_ice_fang_freeze9`, seed 0x5031). The Tri Attack chance draw of 19 has no seed in 300 tries, so the chance is a helper (`src/combat/secondary_rolls.h`, `dfi_tri_attack_chance_hit`) tested at 0, 19, 20 and 99. Power Trip's sum over the seven stats is a helper too (`src/combat/power_trip.h`): no legal user of Power Trip learns Coil, so no battle can carry an accuracy stage on its user. The campaign teams are committed in `tools/cloud/aws_fuzz/campaigns/g44-simple-moves/` (rotated in the cloud runs).

5ao. **G49 (part), Muscle Band, Wise Glasses and Bright Powder** (the item rows of the duration-and-boost batch; provisional letter, the lead relabels at merge). No public value, no new state, no view change, no version bump. **Not part of this step: Damp Rock, Heat Rock, Smooth Rock, Icy Rock and Terrain Extender** (the duration rows). They change the weather and terrain turns to 8, the state's field counter `DFI_FIELD_TURNS_MAX` (5) and the bound of the invariant that reads it, and decision 0018 gives the public `weather_turns` and `terrain_turns` the range up to 8. But the Python live tracker (`python/duoforge_live/tracker.py`, `FIELD_TURNS = 5`) sets and reports the weather and terrain turns at 5 in its observation, so a rock weather would be reported with 5 turns: the owner must decide on that tracker change first (python/duoforge_live is not touched by this step). **Rows, all ENGINE_ROWS** (read by id, no family column: the pool's family columns are the type booster and the resist berry only). Muscle Band (`data/items.ts:4239-4251`) and Wise Glasses (`data/items.ts:7754-7766`): base power x4505/4096 for a Physical move (Muscle Band) or a Special move (Wise Glasses) of the holder, onBasePowerPriority 16, in the chain after Sharpness (19) and before the type boosters (15); the category is the table's (no marked move changes its category at the pin: Shell Side Arm, the one that does, is unmarked, and Tera Blast is not in the pool). Bright Powder (`data/items.ts:659-670`): the accuracy of a move against the holder x3686/4096, onModifyAccuracyPriority -2, in the same event as Wide Lens (priority -2, the attacker's side). The two of priority -2 have no order in the pin (the holders' Speed, a tie draws), so the engine computes both orders and refuses (E_UNSUPPORTED) a move for which they give different modified accuracies: with Compound Eyes (or Snow Cloak and Compound Eyes) before them the chains differ for some base accuracies (68 and 75 with Compound Eyes; 17, 51 and 85 with both), and the test pins those sets. A multi-hit move (the later hits, `multiaccuracy`) refuses a target with Bright Powder, as it refuses Wide Lens. The Champions mod overrides none of the three. The generator checks their pinned texts (`G49_ITEM_FACTS`, `check_g49_facts`) and `tools/datagen/pool_families.js` checks the category probe of the two boosters. The POOL table hash changes (`29c954ab...`) and the POOL fingerprints too (`KP 4a5c4c0b...`, `KPD 60939e2c...`); UNMODELED items 34 to 31. **Evidence** (seven recorded POOL battles, `data: pool`): `g49_wise_glasses` and `g49_wise_glasses_base` (Gholdengo's Shadow Ball at Milotic: 69 damage with the glasses, 63 without, same seed and plan; both Gholdengo, genderless); `g49_muscle_band` and `g49_muscle_band_base` (Kingambit (M)'s Kowtow Cleave at Milotic and Incineroar (M): the band boosts the physical hit, the Leftovers base does not); `g49_bright_powder` and `g49_bright_powder_base` (seed with Raichu (F)'s Thunderbolt drawn at 91: it misses Milotic (F) with the powder, accuracy 90, and hits it with Sitrus Berry; Gholdengo's Shadow Ball, accuracy 100, draws 91 and misses too); `g49_category_cross` (Gholdengo (genderless) holds Muscle Band and casts Shadow Ball, Kingambit (M) holds Wise Glasses and casts Kowtow Cleave: neither item boosts the other category). `g49_bright_powder_tie` (equal Speed of the two holders: the ModifyAccuracy event ties Wide Lens and Bright Powder; `trace_to_c.py` drops that draw under its own rule, `event:ModifyAccuracy` with exactly these two handlers, and refuses any other handler in that event (tie-context)). `tests/test_pool_g49.c` pins the marks, the handler columns, the modifiers and the order sets of the two priority -2 items over the base accuracies 1 to 100, and the two refusals, each with a control that the same battle with the powder removed is accepted: (a) Vivillon with Compound Eyes and Wide Lens, Sleep Powder (accuracy 75, orders 97 and 96) into a Bright Powder holder, a battle built through the step API; (b) Triple Axel's second hit into a Bright Powder holder (the recorded `g33_triple_axel` battle, its first hit's draw is below 81).

**Mutation checks of this step.** 15 mutants of `turn.c`: the thirteen that change a factor, a category, a holder, the powder, or a refusal are caught by a recorded battle or by `test_pool_g49` (a refusal removed, mutants 12 and 13, is caught by the two refusal tests above). Two mutants are **equivalent**, not survivors: (14) Muscle Band's chain placed after the type boosters (priority 15): the only change is its place relative to the type boosters, and one holder carries one item, so Muscle Band and a type booster never apply to the same move; (15) the powder-first order taken for the accuracy: where the engine accepts a move the two orders give the same accuracy, so the value does not change.
5ap. **G46, the drag: Roar, Whirlwind, Dragon Tail, Circle Throw, Red Card, and the DragOut blockers Suction Cups and Guard Dog** (the forced switch of the hit, the phazing after each action, and the draw over the non-fainted bench, `DFI_SITE_DRAG`; the public event DRAG (45), a switch of the drag kind; `party_order` of section 7 is its state, own side exact and foe side from the hypothesis's brought order, decision 0023). Rows marked: Roar, Whirlwind, Dragon Tail, Circle Throw, Red Card, Suction Cups, Guard Dog; Substitute, Mold Breaker, Sheer Force, Shell Bell, Pickpocket and Skill Swap stay unmarked, each with a guard line in `test_pool_tables.c`. The drag-in DragOut re-check is equivalent while no marked row changes a blocker mid-turn (Skill Swap would make it live). Evidence: 15 `g46_*` battles of the pinned Showdown (no-reserve Roar and Red Card, multi-hit Red Card, Emergency Exit and Eject Button against a drag, a fainted reserve in the draw), 14 mutations of the drag (10 caught; 4 equivalent, with the reasons in the PR), and a 300-battle random differential campaign (seed 20261009) that passes 300 of 300 after the fixes it found (move-caused faints before the phazing, Life Orb after a Red Card drag, the converter's entry order). Version: none; HauptSession assigns it.

5aq. **I1, Illusion's infrastructure: POOL tail rev 5 (0x0503) and the causes of a public refusal (decision 0026)**. No mechanic and no recorded battle; the owner's public values of 0026 section 6 that this step carries.
   - **Tail rev 5.** The rev 4 part is unchanged (288 bytes); after it come 60 bytes: per side the Illusion state (18 bytes: `shown`, `override[4]`, `snapshot[9]`, `pending[4]`), per position two lane A bytes (`slot_pending`, `future_sight`), then 16 bytes of reserve. Nothing writes them: every byte is zero, and any other value is refused (`TAIL_SIDE` for the Illusion state, `TAIL_POSITION` for the position bytes, `TAIL_RESERVED` for the reserve). The state is 1357 bytes under the POOL kinds; schema 0x0503 is the only one accepted there, and 0x0403 is refused since rev 5. CLOSURE and TEAM_C are byte for byte unchanged.
   - **The public record grows.** The state array of `duoforge_public_state` holds the POOL state, so `DUOFORGE_VIEW_STATE_MAX` is 1357 and the record is 1396 bytes (was 1336). This is the size of the canonical state, not a new field; decision 0026 section 3 says that no view struct grows, which the lead should weigh against this.
   - **The causes call (C only).** `duoforge_battle_public_causes` and `duoforge_batch_public_causes` write the mask of the causes that the player's view decides: `DUOFORGE_PUBLIC_CAUSE_VISIBLE_SLEEP` 1 and `_VISIBLE_CONFUSION` 2 (the test of `dfi_view_counter_support`, now one predicate with the refusal of `duoforge_battle_public`), `_ILLUSION_POSSIBLE` 4, which stays 0 until Illusion. A mask of 0 with a refusing record means another refusal.
   - **Evidence.** The byte sweep of `tests/test_pool_tail.c` covers all 348 tail bytes against the state model (`state_v3_model.py --pool-tail`); the in-memory cases cover the new fields. Targeted tests: `duoforge.state.pool_tail`, `duoforge.view`, `duoforge.view.causes` (sleep only, confusion only, both, neither, a bench sleep seen by its own side only, a PIVOT refusal with mask 0, hidden counters and RNG, the arguments, the batch for 1 to 16 workers), `duoforge.python.layout`, `replay` and `live`. Mutation checks, 9 of 9 caught: the record ignores the causes, the causes call drops the confusion bit, encode/decode/invariant each skipping one rev 5 byte, the reserve check skipping its last byte. Campaign: `diff_driver.py random`, seed 20261009, 50 battles over teams D and E of mega-batch2 with A (POOL): PASS 50, DIVERGENCE 0, UNSUPPORTED 0.
   - **Version.** No bump in this step; the MINOR is assigned at merge.
   - **Campaign**: `diff_driver.py random`, seed 4811, 300 battles, pairings XY, YX, XX, YY over two G48 teams (X: Annihilape, Kleavor, Maushold, Samurott-Hisui, Gholdengo, Milotic; Y: Kingambit and the foes of the G48 specs), data POOL, domain rate 0.1, harness 14 (pin b2cb775). PASS 298, UNSUPPORTED 2, DIVERGENCE 0, ORACLE_GAP 0, REF_ERROR 0, CAP 0. The 1764 sampled requests: 1755 offered what Showdown accepts (the battles that ran to the end), none dropped. The two UNSUPPORTED battles (`fz_4811_86`, `fz_4811_289`) stop in the residual phase, where a Pokemon faints from burn damage in a tie of residual entries (`dfi_residual_order_ambiguous`, an existing refusal); no G48 move runs in those steps. The teams carry the four G48 moves; how often random play used each one was not counted (the traces were not kept: `--keep-traces` was not passed).

5ar. **G50, Double Shock** (decision 0025 items 1, 2 and 5; `data/moves.ts:3945-3969`, the Champions punch flag at `data/mods/champions/moves.ts:256-259`). Marked for Pawmot (Electric and Fighting, Iron Fist) only, the one usable Pawmot; every other shape is refused with `E_UNSUPPORTED` (tests in `tests/test_pool_g50.c`). The lead approved the public values: `DUOFORGE_TYPE_NONE` = 255 (the typeless first slot, `???`) and the `TYPE_CHANGE` amount = the second type id + 1 (0 = one type, which Soak keeps). Internal: `soak_type` may hold `DFI_TAIL_TYPE2_TYPELESS` (255) only with a nonzero `type2` (invariant TAIL_MEMBER); `dfi_types_of` reads the typeless slot as `DFI_CLOSURE_NONE`, which every reader skips; the view maps it to `type_now` = {0, type2 + 1}.
   - **The pin**: Double Shock fails without the Electric type (`-fail` and `[still]`); after a hit the user's types are `???` and the second type (`-start|X|typechange|???/Fighting|[from] move: Double Shock`, the event TYPE_CHANGE with detail 255 and amount 6). A switch-out resets both, Soak (`setType`, `sim/pokemon.ts:2109`) replaces both with Water and clears the second type, and a Ground move hits the typeless Pawmot neutrally (Fighting, not Electric).
   - **No target**: Showdown runs TryMove before the no-targets test (`sim/battle-actions.ts:486-513`) and its target is not null when the foe in slot 0 has fainted, so a Double Shock with nothing to hit fails with `-fail` and `[still]` when the user lacks Electric. The campaign below found it: every one of its six divergences was this case, and `dfi_no_target_or_try` now runs the gate first (pinned by `g50_double_shock_no_target`). **Known pin-order question (not changed in this item, a later check):** Showdown runs TryMove before the no-targets test (`sim/battle-actions.ts:486-513`), but the engine returns the no-target result first for every move with nothing to hit; only Double Shock's gate was moved ahead of it. Other moves whose TryMove gate can fail (Fake Out's first-turn rule, Sucker Punch's target move, and any other onTry fail) would print the no-target line where the pin prints the gate's own `-fail`. No recorded battle of this item covers those moves, and this item does not change them.
    - **Tables**: after G44's four handlers (ids 67 to 70, merged from main, after G48's 63 to 66), DOUBLE_SHOCK = 71 and UNMODELED = 72; the tables are regenerated by `gen_closure.py --pool`.
   - **Evidence**: six recorded POOL battles (`g50_double_shock_fail`, `_switch`, `_soak`, `_ground`, `_soak_after`: Double Shock, then Soak on the typeless Pawmot, and `_no_target`), genders stated; with G48's eleven, 375 pool battles in the conformance and tiebreak data (2185 stops); `duoforge.state.pool_g50` checks the soak type and second type after every step of the first five, the two refused shapes, the view and a byte-equal codec round trip with soak type 255.
   - **Mutation checks**: eleven one-change mutants of `src/combat/turn.c`, `src/state/invariants.c` and `src/state/observation.c` (`g50_mutate.py`): the typeless STAB slot, the Electric gate, the self effect, the amount and the detail of TYPE_CHANGE, the view's two slots, the invariant, the special whitelist, Soak's clearing of the second type, and the no-target gate. Eleven of eleven are caught, by `duoforge.state.pool_g50`, `duoforge.state.pool_tail`, `duoforge.reference.conformance_pool_data` or both. M01 to M10 were run before the no-target fix and are not run again after it (the fix does not change their edited lines); M02 and M11 were run after it.
   - **Campaign**: `diff_driver.py random`, seed 5050, 300 battles, pairings XY, YX, XX, YY, Pawmot teams (X: Pawmot with Iron Fist against Y: Garchomp, Samurott-Hisui, Milotic, Gholdengo, Primarina, Sneasler). The first run gave 294 PASS and 6 DIVERGENCE (all the no-target Double Shock above); after the fix the same seed gives 300 PASS, with 1639 of 1639 sampled requests offered by Showdown.
   - **Version**: no version bump here; it comes at merge.
5as. **G42, Roost and Stomping Tantrum** (no new public value: the view bit 40 (ROOST) that decision 0018 names is supported, bit 41 (MOVE_FAILED) is not; no version bump). Roost (`data/moves.ts:15428-15463`) heals the user (the heal of step G10) and then takes the self volatile `roost` (duration 1, residual order 25, `data/moves.ts:15444-15455`): its `onType` removes the Flying type after the other types. An empty list is Normal from gen 5 (`sim/pokemon.ts:2143`), so a pure Flying Pokemon is Normal for the turn. The self volatile is applied only to a target that did not fail (`sim/battle-actions.ts:1088-1096`), so a user at full HP gets a false result and no volatile. Stomping Tantrum (`data/moves.ts:18054-18058`) doubles its base power when the user's `moveLastTurnResult` is `false`, strictly: null is no failure.
  - **Tail.** The Roost volatile is the single_turn bit `DFI_SINGLE_TURN_ROOST` (rev 4, layout unchanged), set at the heal, cleared by the residual of order 25 (silent), and read by `dfi_types_of`, so the Flying filter follows the volatile.
  - **The classifier.** `dfi_run_move` records the pin's `moveThisTurnResult` of each action in `mres`. The pin's rule (`sim/battle-actions.ts` `useMove`): the result is the return value of `useMoveInner`, unless the action itself assigned the value (null for NOT_FAIL, false for `beforeMove`, `nopp` and a `beforeMoveCallback`). In the engine every exit of the move body writes a class: **TRUE** when the effect applied (a damaging hit, a status or boost that changed, a heal, a side, field, weather or terrain that is set, Trick Room, Perish Song with one holder, Wide Guard, Imprison, Helping Hand, Coaching, Follow Me, Rage Powder, Aurora Veil, a Protect that goes up, a pivot's hit); **FALSE** for a `[still]` failure (`dfi_fail_still`: the volatile or condition is already up, Protect repeated and failing, a stall roll, Steel Roller off its terrain, a first-turn-only move, a Sucker Punch against a status move), a miss, an immunity, a Good as Gold stop, an ally that will not move, a boost that changes nothing, Armor Tail's and the TryMove stops (`cant`), `cant nopp`, and no target (`[notarget]`); **NULL** for a move that Protect or Wide Guard took (their `TryHit` returns NOT_FAIL, `data/moves.ts:13998`, `:20838-20848`), a recharge stop, and the charge turn of a two-turn move (its `onTryMove` returns null, `data/moves.ts` solarbeam). The two bits of an action move to bit 5 at the end of its turn (`dfi_end_turn`, `sim/battle.ts:1674-1675`). **UNCLASSIFIED** (bit 4 this turn, bit 5 the last turn, both result values zero) is written only when no exit writes a class: a static audit of every exit of the move body (the list in the PR body) finds none, so the bit is the safety net of `dfi_run_move` and no battle or campaign has it. Stomping Tantrum doubles only on a FALSE last result and refuses (E_UNSUPPORTED) on the last unclassified bit.
  - **Enumeration.** The 200 marked moves by the class of their exit (the counts of the support manifest against the pool rows): damaging with secondary, recoil, drain or plain 116 (among them the pivots U-turn, Volt Switch and Flip Turn); damaging with a handler rule 16; damaging with a condition 6 (Fake Out, First Impression, Feint, Sucker Punch, Sacred Sword, Darkest Lariat); damaging multi-hit or spread 3; the protect family 3 (Protect, Detect, Spiky Shield); self boosts 9 (Coil, Swords Dance, Calm Mind, Nasty Plot, Agility, Bulk Up, Dragon Dance, Quiver Dance, Iron Defense); heal 3 (Recover, Roost, Life Dew); boosts of the target 2 (Charm, Fake Tears); ally support 2 (Coaching, Helping Hand); primary status to target 6 (Hypnosis, Poison Powder, Sleep Powder, Stun Spore, Toxic, Will-O-Wisp); volatile or item effect to target 11 (Covet, Disable, Encore, Imprison, Parting Shot, Soak, Switcheroo, Taunt, Thief, Trick, Yawn); side conditions 7 (Light Screen, Reflect, Tailwind, Spikes, Stealth Rock, Sticky Web, Toxic Spikes); Aurora Veil 1; Wide Guard 1; weather 4; terrain 5; Trick Room 1; Perish Song 1; redirection 2 (Follow Me, Rage Powder); charge 1 (Solar Beam).
  - **Changed in the review.** The status effect that changes nothing is FALSE (it was unclassified); a self boost that succeeds is TRUE (Coil was unclassified); side, hazard, weather, terrain, Trick Room, Imprison, Wide Guard, Aurora Veil, Perish Song and the Helping Hand and Coaching successes are TRUE; Armor Tail's stop, `cant nopp`, Good as Gold's stops and a boost that changes nothing are FALSE; the charge turn is NULL.
  - **Refusals of the first campaign.** Three step refusals (fz_4242_61 at step 35, fz_4242_62 at step 20, fz_4242_102 at step 14) were identified with a diagnostic build (`turn.c` printing each E_UNSUPPORTED site; reverted): all three come from `dfi_residual_pair_draws` ("a bigger group: its shuffle is not modelled"): three or more holders of equal Speed whose no-order residual handlers (Protect's volatile and the stall counter) tie at the end of a turn. That refusal is on origin/main and is the limit of decision 0015 G26; it is not a G42 refusal, and none of the three is the unclassified refusal of Stomping Tantrum.
  - **Roost under Heal Block** is not reachable: Heal Block is not a row of the legal pool. **A pure Flying Roost user** is not reachable either: no legal Champions pure Flying Pokemon learns Roost (the Normal rule of `dfi_types_of` is the reasoning above).
  - **The view.** `POSITION_EXT_ROOST` (volatiles bit 20; supported bit 40) is set while the Roost turn is open: only at a boundary inside the turn, the pivot of `g42_roost_pivot`, since the residual ends the volatile at every turn end. `move_failed` is 1 iff the last result is FALSE. Bit 41 stays clear in `dfi_support.view_ext_features` (the decision is the lead's; the safety net of the unclassified bit is not yet shown never to fire in the field).
  - **Evidence.** Eleven recorded POOL battles (genders are in the specs, each checked with `ps_trace.js --check`): `g42_roost_full` (Roost at full HP fails; Stomping Tantrum doubled after the failure; a Tantrum blocked by Protect is null and not doubled after it), `g42_tantrum_switch` (the Tantrum after a switch-in is not doubled), `g42_roost_heal` (a Roost at less than full HP: the heal, the Flying type off), `g42_tantrum_paralysis` (a full paralysis `cant` on turn 1; turn 2 doubled), `g42_tantrum_miss` (a Rock Tomb miss; turn 2 doubled), `g42_roost_ground_order` (a Ground move before the Roost is immune, after it hits, the next turn immune again; a second cycle), `g42_roost_pivot` (a U-turn after the Roost in the same turn: the pivot boundary shows the volatile, the pivoting position keeps its results until its replacement switches in), `g42_protect_tantrum` (Protect on turn 1 is TRUE, the Tantrum of turn 2 is not doubled), `g42_protect_stall_tantrum` (Protect on turn 1 TRUE, a repeated Protect fails FALSE on turn 2, the turn-3 Tantrum is doubled), `g42_tailwind_tantrum` (Tailwind TRUE, Tantrum not doubled, a repeated Tailwind FALSE, the next Tantrum doubled), `g42_coil_tantrum` (Coil TRUE on each turn, the Tantrums after it not doubled).
  - **Tests.** `tests/test_pool_g42.c` (`duoforge.state.pool_g42`): at each of the 196 boundary positions of the eleven battles, the two-bit `this` and `last` values, the unclassified bits and the ROOST bit, against a table derived from the pinned protocol lines (TRUE, FALSE, NULL and UNDEFINED as above; a faint or a switch-out clears a position; a pivot boundary shows the turn so far); the view of both viewers after each step; the unclassified refusal (bit 5 set on the Dragonite of `g42_tantrum_switch` before its Stomping Tantrum: E_UNSUPPORTED); the support mask (bit 40 yes, bit 41 no); the invariant check after every step. The conformance and tiebreak pool data run the eleven battles (335 pool battles, 1934 tiebreak stops). Counts updated: `test_pool_tables` (151 marked moves in the pool tables, the closure ids excluded), `test_observation_ext` (the mask), `tools/reference/test_trace_to_c.py`, `tools/datagen/test_gen_closure.py` (ROOST 63, STOMPING_TANTRUM 64, UNMODELED 65). `tools/reference/trace_to_c.py` takes the `roost` volatile into IGNORED_VOLATILES.
  - **Mutation.** Eleven mutants of the rules, each caught by a targeted test: M1 Roost sets no volatile; M2 the volatile is set at full HP; M3 the move result survives the occupant's leaving; M4 Stomping Tantrum doubles on null and on undefined; M5 the turn end does not shift this turn into last; M6 the Roost bit is never cleared; M7 no Flying filter; M8 a miss is classified null; M9 a successful status or self boost is not TRUE; M10 a set side condition is not TRUE; U the unclassified refusal of Stomping Tantrum is removed. Eleven of eleven caught (the list of targets is in the PR body).
  - **Campaigns.** (a) 300 random POOL battles, seed 4242, teams D and E (the first run had Sand Force, which is not modelled, and was rerun with Sand Rush): PASS 297, UNSUPPORTED 3 (the step refusals above), no DIVERGENCE; domain 1240 of 1260 agreed. (b) 300 random POOL battles, seed 4343, teams F and G (Protect and Stomping Tantrum users, Dragonite with Roost and Stomping Tantrum): PASS 300, UNSUPPORTED 0, no DIVERGENCE; domain 1089 of 1089 agreed. Unclassified refusals counted separately: 0 in both. Team F and G are composed sets from the legal pool (the registry's 654 real teams hold no Stomping Tantrum or Roost): they are not ladder pulls.
  - **Not in this step.** Roost under Heal Block and the pure-Flying Normal case (above).
5ba. **G45, six abilities of the simple kind: Steadfast, Moxie, Weak Armor, Telepathy, Volt Absorb and Punk Rock** (the next free letter after G43 5am and G49 5an; no public value, no new event, no view change, no version bump, no new draw site, no battle state). Pressure is **not** in this step (decision B of the lead, 2026-10-09): its PP charge is not in any protocol line, so the opponent's derived PP would differ from the reference; it is parked for a later step that derives the charge from public lines (the observation fold). All six are engine rows (`ENGINE_ROWS` of the generator, handler NONE) that `src/combat/turn.c` reads by id, and they are marked in `src/data/support_manifest.c`:
   - **Steadfast** (`data/abilities.ts:4549-4557`, onFlinch: `boost({spe: 1})`, the holder its own source): after the `[cant] flinch` line of a flinched move, `-ability|holder|Steadfast|boost`, then `-boost`; the ability's primary change (a change of 0 shows nothing).
   - **Moxie** (`:2749-2759`, onSourceAfterFaint: `boost({atk: length}, source)` when the last faint's effect is a Move). `length` is the number of faints that the faintMessages call shows (`sim/battle.ts:2537-2601`); AfterFaint runs after the faint lines and the win check, so a finished battle gets nothing. **Run-scoped scratch, not state:** the engine keeps the cause of the last queued faint in two fields of `dfi_run` (`last_faint_by`, `last_faint_move`), set by the hit loop when a Move's direct damage queues a faint. They are set and read inside one step's run, between the faint announcement and AfterFaint (or the process of the same action). Every action's epilogue runs `dfi_process_faints` before a step can end, a PIVOT boundary included, which resets `faint_count`; so the fields are dead at a step boundary. They are never saved in the battle, the codec or the digest.
   - **Weak Armor** (`:5448-5458`, onDamagingHit: a Physical move, every hit of a multi-hit move: `boost({def: -1, spe: 2}, target, target)`, the holder its own source). It sits in the DamagingHit loop after Justified and before the attacker's Poison Touch; a fainted holder gets nothing.
   - **Telepathy** (`:4931-4942`, onTryHit, breakable: a non-Status move of an ally, target not the user: `-activate|holder|ability: Telepathy`, the hit is stopped for that target). A spread move still hits the others.
   - **Volt Absorb** (`:5340-5353`, onTryHit, breakable: an Electric move of another Pokemon, a Status move included): `this.heal(baseMaxhp / 4)` whose source is the user (`-heal|holder|hp|[from] ability: Volt Absorb|[of] user`), and when the heal does nothing (full HP or Heal Block) `-immune|holder|[from] ability: Volt Absorb`; the move is stopped for that target either way. It is the TryHit loop's branch after Good as Gold's, the Lightning Rod path.
   - **Punk Rock** (`:3589-3602`, breakable): onBasePowerPriority 7, `chainModify([5325, 4096])` for a sound move of the holder, placed after Helping Hand (10) and the items (15), before the terrains (6); onSourceModifyDamage, `chainModify(0.5)` for a sound move against the holder, a target's ability in the ModifyDamage chain (one of Solid Rock, Multiscale, Aura Guard and Punk Rock per hit, so the chain still has six modifiers at most).
   - **Breakable.** Telepathy, Volt Absorb and Punk Rock are breakable. No marked ability ignores that flag: Mold Breaker is unmarked (`tests/test_pool_g45.c` pins it), Teravolt, Turboblaze and Mycelium Might are not in the pool, and a move with `ignoreAbility` is UNMODELED. So no interaction is implemented and none is refused.
   - **Evidence**: six recorded POOL battles (genders stated in each paste): `g45_steadfast` (Persian (F)'s Fake Out flinches Machamp (M): `cant|flinch`, `-ability|Steadfast|boost`, `-boost|spe|1`); `g45_moxie_double` (Krookodile (M)'s Earthquake knocks out Pikachu (M) and Raichu (F) at once: two faints, then `-ability|Moxie|boost` and `-boost|atk|2`); `g45_weak_armor` (Garchomp (M)'s Earthquake on Ceruledge (M): `-ability|Weak Armor|boost`, `-unboost|def|1`, `-boost|spe|2`); `g45_telepathy` (Garchomp (M)'s Earthquake reaches Medicham (M), stopped by Telepathy, the two foes take it); `g45_volt_absorb` (Raichu (F)'s Thunderbolt at Jolteon (M) at full HP is `-immune`; after Garchomp's Earthquake the Thunderbolt heals it, `[from] ability: Volt Absorb|[of] Raichu`); `g45_punk_rock` (Hyper Voice of Toxtricity (M) and Toxtricity (F), both with Punk Rock; damage in both directions, checked by the conformance comparison).
   - **Rework evidence** (five more POOL battles, recorded after the lead's rework request; the specs in `tests/reference/specs/`, the traces in `tests/reference/traces/`): `g45_weak_armor_special` (Gholdengo (genderless) Shadow Ball at Ceruledge (M), three times: no Weak Armor line and no stage change on the holder); `g45_telepathy_status` (Gholdengo's Shadow Ball is a foe move and hits Medicham (M), the Telepathy holder; Pikachu (M)'s Charm, a Status move from the ally, is not stopped); `g45_volt_absorb_heal_block` (Farigiraf (F)'s Psychic Noise on Jolteon (M), then Raichu (F)'s Thunderbolt: `-immune` with `[from] ability: Volt Absorb`, not a heal, because Heal Block is on); `g45_punk_rock_attacker` (Toxtricity (M)'s Hyper Voice, a sound move of the holder, against two foes without Punk Rock); `g45_moxie_win` (Krookodile (M)'s Earthquake knocks out two Electric foes on turn 1, Moxie +2; one more on turn 3, Moxie +1; the last foe on turn 4 ends the battle with no Moxie line, because the win comes before AfterFaint).
   - **Tests**: `tests/test_pool_g45.c` (`duoforge.state.pool_g45`): the marks and the handler column, the Mold Breaker family unmarked, and the state after the recorded battles: Steadfast's Speed stage, Moxie's Attack stage and the two faints, Weak Armor's stages, Telepathy's HP, Volt Absorb's full HP and its heal (a quarter, then Leftovers a sixteenth). The conformance tests compare every event of the six battles with Showdown.
   - **Mutation checks**: **24 of 28 caught** (the edits of `turn.c` built and run against `duoforge.state.pool_g45` and the pool conformance tests, each restored after, on the merged tree with the eleven battles). Caught: Steadfast dropped and +2 Speed; Moxie dropped, +1, twice the length, without the announce path; Weak Armor dropped, Defense -2, Speed +1, and for special moves too; Telepathy dropped, for foes too, for Status moves too, and the line without the stop; Volt Absorb dropped, a third, without -immune, with Heal Block ignored, and the heal without its source; Punk Rock's BasePower dropped, x1.5, its weakening dropped, x0.75, and the attacker weakened instead.
     Survivors, with the reason. **S3** Steadfast in SELF mode: equivalent (the two differ only at a change of 0, which shows nothing). **M4** Moxie without the process path: equivalent for every reachable battle (a move's direct faints are announced in its hit loop; argued, not proven). **M6** Moxie with the win check dropped: equivalent in this engine. After a finished battle `dfi_boost` returns at its `foes_left == 0` guard before any stage change, so the win check and that guard are redundant; `g45_moxie_win` shows no Moxie line after the win, and with the guard in place the mutant cannot change it. **V6** Volt Absorb absorbing Status Electric moves: not catchable, no marked Electric status move targets a Pokemon (Thunder Wave is unmarked, so no battle can contain one). Reported, not hidden.
   - **Campaign** `tools/cloud/aws_fuzz/campaigns/g45-abilities/` (`G.txt` holds the six new holders, `H.txt` Steadfast and three fillers; 312 battles over twelve pairings with the teams A and B): `tools/cloud/aws_fuzz/campaigns/g45-abilities/` (`G.txt` holds the six new holders, `H.txt` Steadfast and three fillers): `diff_driver.py random --seed 4500 --battles 312` over the twelve pairings GG, GH, HG, HH, GA, AG, HA, AH, GB, BG, HB, BH (the teams A and B are the defaults): **312 of 312 PASS**, 1371 domain requests sampled and all agreed, no UNSUPPORTED, no ORACLE_GAP. The campaign's results are not committed (the run is outside the repository).
5bc. **G51, Mega batch 4: ten Mega Stones, mark only, and the two base abilities of Pidgeot, Keen Eye and Big Pecks** (no public value, no new state, no view change, no version bump; the library version stays what main has). The stones are Chandelurite (Chandelure-Mega, Infiltrator), Houndoominite (Houndoom-Mega, Solar Power), Lucarionite (Lucario-Mega, Adaptability; not the Z stone of Mega batch 2), Dragalgite (Dragalge-Mega, Regenerator), Crabominite (Crabominable-Mega, Iron Fist), Chimechite (Chimecho-Mega, Levitate), Glalitite (Glalie-Mega, Refrigerate), Pinsirite (Pinsir-Mega, Aerilate), Banettite (Banette-Mega, Prankster) and Pidgeotite (Pidgeot-Mega, No Guard).
   - **The pin.** Each stone is a `megaStone` item of `data/items.ts` (Banettite 380, Chandelurite 762, Chimechite 939, Crabominite 1215, Dragalgite 1471, Glalitite 2497, Houndoominite 2902, Lucarionite 3498, Pidgeotite 4558, Pinsirite 4605) with one Mega each, and `inherit: true, isNonstandard: null` in `data/mods/champions/items.ts` (74, 134, 150, 186, 222, 390, 470, 550, 678, 682). The Mega formes (`data/pokedex.ts`): Banette-Mega 6612, Chandelure-Mega 11239, Chimecho-Mega 6680, Crabominable-Mega 13977, Dragalge-Mega 12999, Glalie-Mega 6777, Houndoom-Mega 4692, Lucario-Mega 8243, Pidgeot-Mega 396, Pinsir-Mega 2868. The Champions mod overrides none of their abilities, except Regenerator (`data/mods/champions/abilities.ts:63-70`: the switch-out heal of a third, `[silent]`, which the engine's own Regenerator row already follows).
   - **The support.** The Mega abilities were marked already (Infiltrator, Solar Power, Adaptability, Regenerator, Iron Fist, Levitate, Refrigerate, Aerilate, Prankster, No Guard). The base abilities of the pairs are marked and legal for their base formes (`dfi_pool_forme_legal`): Flash Fire (Chandelure, Houndoom), Inner Focus (Lucario, Glalie), Adaptability (Dragalge), Hyper Cutter (Crabominable, Pinsir), Levitate (Chimecho), Cursed Body (Banette), Keen Eye and Big Pecks (Pidgeot).
   - **Keen Eye and Big Pecks** are engine rows (`ENGINE_ROWS` of both generators; the handler column of the pool tables goes from UNMODELED to NONE). Pin: Keen Eye `data/abilities.ts:2260-2276` (`onTryBoost`: an accuracy drop that another Pokemon causes is deleted after Contrary and the cap, with the line `-fail|X|unboost|accuracy|[from] ability: Keen Eye|[of] X` unless the effect is a move's secondary; `onModifyMove`: `move.ignoreEvasion = true` for the holder's own moves; breakable), Big Pecks `data/abilities.ts:445-458` (the same for a Defense drop, `[from] ability: Big Pecks`, not Octolock; breakable). The Champions mod overrides neither. Engine (`src/combat/turn.c`): a Keen Eye or Big Pecks block in `dfi_boost`, the Hyper Cutter shape (`veil[STAGE]`, `source != flat`, `capped < BIAS6`, the event `FAIL` with the ability as its id); and `dfi_accuracy_check` reads `ignoreEvasion` for a Keen Eye user, as it does for Darkest Lariat. No Mold Breaker is marked, so no move or ability ignores either. Neither holder can meet another handler of its stat (one ability per holder; no Grass forme has either ability: `tests/test_pool_g51.c` checks it). The generator facts (`G51_ABILITY_FACTS`, checked by `check_g34_facts`) pin both callbacks and the breakable flags. The converter (`trace_to_c.py`) accepts `accuracy` only with Keen Eye and `def` only with Big Pecks (the id checked).
   - **Reach.** The accuracy block is reached through Muddy Water's 30 percent accuracy secondary (Muddy Water is marked and learnable: Milotic and Dragalge), the Defense block through Crunch's 20 percent Defense secondary (marked, learnable: Incineroar, Houndoom, Garchomp). Nothing else of the marked moves lowers accuracy or Defense of a foe (the accuracy droppers are unmarked, and the marked self-droppers are not drops by another Pokemon). A marked move that raises evasion does not exist, so the ignoreEvasion half is not reachable in a recorded battle: `tests/test_pool_g51.c` (`check_keen_eye_evasion`) raises the foe's evasion stage by six by hand in the Keen Eye battle, where the Pidgeot's Hurricane (accuracy 70) draws 31 and hits, which a holder without the ability would miss.
   - **Evidence.** Twelve recorded POOL battles (`tests/reference/traces/g51_*`, gender stated for every gendered Pokemon in the paste): `g51_chandelurite` (Chandelure (F): Light Screen of the foe Milotic (F) on turn 1, the Mega on turn 1, Flamethrower into the screen on turn 2); `g51_houndoominite` (Houndoom (M): the Mega on turn 1, Incineroar (M)'s Sunny Day, Solar Power's `[from] ability: Solar Power` damage in the sun on the end of turn 1); `g51_lucarionite` (Lucario (M): the Mega on turn 1, Close Combat on turn 2, the base Inner Focus refusing Intimidate's Attack drop); `g51_dragalgite` (Dragalge (F): the Mega on turn 1, Flip Turn on turn 2, the `[silent]` Regenerator heal at the switch-out); `g51_crabominite` (Crabominable (M): Hyper Cutter refuses Intimidate on the base, the Mega on turn 1, Ice Punch into Raichu on turn 3); `g51_chimechite` (Chimecho (F): the Mega on turn 1, Garchomp's Earthquake `-immune` from Levitate); `g51_glalitite` (Glalie (M): the Mega on turn 1, Giga Impact on turn 2, `-supereffective` on Garchomp (M) by Refrigerate); `g51_pinsirite` (Pinsir (M): the Mega on turn 1, Quick Attack and Close Combat under Aerilate); `g51_banettite` (Banette (F): Will-O-Wisp before the faster Sneasler on turn 1, Prankster's priority); `g51_pidgeotite` (Pidgeot (M): the Mega on turn 1, Hurricane on turns 2 and 3 with no accuracy draw, No Guard); `g51_keen_eye` (Pidgeot (M, Keen Eye): Muddy Water's accuracy secondary on the living Pidgeot is refused, seed 0x7101, the Hurricane of step 2 draws 31); `g51_big_pecks` (Pidgeot (M, Big Pecks): Crunch's Defense secondary on the living Pidgeot is refused, seed 0x7206). Genders: every gendered species of the paste is named with its sex in the spec file. The two stone battles that first failed the pool setup (Glalie and Pinsir with Double-Edge: not in the pool's legal learnset for either forme, which the Showdown validator's base learnsets accept) were re-planned with Giga Impact and Quick Attack.
   - **Tests.** `duoforge.state.pool_g51` (new, white-box): the marks (the ten stones with handler NONE, Keen Eye and Big Pecks marked engine rows, the Mega and base abilities marked and legal), no Grass forme with either ability, the Mega of each pair (Lucarionite gives Lucario-Mega, Lucarionite Z gives Lucario-Mega-Z), the setup, the API, and the Keen Eye ignoreEvasion check. `tests/test_pool_tables.c`: the stone predicate lists the ten ids, the engine predicate adds Keen Eye and Big Pecks, `UNMODELED_ABILITIES` 137 to 135 (both are engine rows), and `POOL_HASH_HEX` moves to the new canonical hash (sha256 `290fc1be…`, from `2515ab6a…`: the handler column changed). `tests/test_pool_setup.c`: the two POOL fingerprints (KP `97de7047…`, KPD `53d50091…`) follow the new tables. `tests/CMakeLists.txt`: the provenance hash of `pool_tables.c` (`783ca53e…`) and the registration of `duoforge.state.pool_g51`. `tests/test_conformance.c`: 336 POOL battles (324 plus twelve). `tests/test_tiebreak.c`: 336 battles and 1936 stops (62 more). `duoforge.python.replay` and `duoforge.python.live` pass.
   - **Mutation.** Twenty-five mutants, each built and run against `duoforge.state.pool_g51`, `reference.conformance_pool_data`, `reference.tiebreak_pool_data`, `data.pool_tables` and `state.pool_setup`: ten un-markings of the stones (10 of 10 caught), and fifteen of the rules: Keen Eye's veil removed, its source check removed (survived), its boost check removed (survived: equivalent, a zero boost never lowers a stat), its cap `<=` (survived: a drop from -6 is not in any battle), the wrong ability in its condition, the veil on Defense, the ignoreEvasion removed (caught by the white-box test), the FAIL event with another id (survived: no battle prints a Keen Eye line outside a secondary), the secondary exemption removed; the same for Big Pecks: veil removed, veil on Attack, source check removed (survived: no Pidgeot drop of its own Defense), boost check removed (survived: equivalent), wrong ability, secondary exemption removed. **19 of 25 caught**; survivors are the six above, each explained (four unreachable in a recorded battle, two equivalent). The Big Pecks battle had first been recorded with another seed, where all six Big Pecks mutants survived: the seed was then changed to one with a single secondary roll on the living holder.
   - **Review rework (the lead's review of PR #281).** (1) The pin's test is `source && target === source` (data/abilities.ts:2262 for Keen Eye, :447 for Big Pecks): only a drop the holder causes itself is let through. In the engine `DFI_POSITIONS` is the holder itself (the comment of `dfi_boost`), `source == flat` is the holder too, and a drop by another Pokemon is blocked, as the pin does. The pin's third case, a drop with no source at all, cannot be written in the engine (the source is a position or `DFI_POSITIONS`) and cannot occur for these stats in the pool: the pin's negative Attack, Defense and accuracy boosts without a source are the Max moves (Max Phantasm, data/moves.ts:11350, and the boost at :11484), `isNonstandard: "Past"`, not in Champions. The condition is therefore left exactly as it is, with the reasoning in the comment of the block. Hyper Cutter (G39) has the same shape and the same reasoning, so it is not changed. (2) The six survivors, by reason. The source checks (Keen Eye on accuracy, Big Pecks on Defense) are unreachable: no legal Keen Eye forme lowers its own accuracy, and the only Big Pecks forme, Pidgeot, learns no move that lowers its own Defense (the Keen Eye formes that learn Close Combat, Farfetch'd and Lycanroc, lose their own Defense and Special Defense, which Keen Eye does not block). The cap at -6 (`<=`) and the FAIL event id are unreachable: a primary accuracy or Defense drop caused by another Pokemon is in no marked move, so the non-secondary branch never runs. The two boost checks are equivalent: a zero boost cannot lower a stat, so the veil changes nothing. A white-box test through the step API would need a legal primary drop or a legal self drop, and `dfi_boost` is static, so these four cannot be closed without a new test hook or a marked move; they are left as survivors with these reasons. Caught stays 19 of 25 (the engine code is unchanged since the mutation run, only comments moved).
   - **Regenerator on a pivot switch-out (a fix of G39 Regenerator on a pivot switch-out, examined; no engine change).** `duoforge.python.replay` (`SpectatorTest.test_view_equals_duoforge`) reported `53 != 20` for `g51_dragalgite` at k=3, side 0 (Dragalge-Mega, Regenerator, leaves by Flip Turn). The engine is correct. A white-box test (`check_regenerator_pivot` in `tests/test_pool_g51.c`) checks the state at the pivot request, after step 2: Dragalge has not left and has not healed, its own HP is 35 of 172 and the opponent's view is 20 percent. After the answer (`switch 3`) the heal of a third (57, to 92 of 172) comes first, and the opponent's view is 53 percent: the HEAL event (cause ABILITY, Regenerator) reaches the fold, which attributes it to the leaving member. The mismatch is in the spectator's point placement, `python/duoforge_replay/points.py`: a switch point is the `|switch|` line, and the observation is the fold of the lines before it, so for a pivot whose switch-out heals, the spectator's point includes the heal (`-heal ... [from] ability: Regenerator|[silent]` precedes `|switch|p1a: Staraptor|...|[from] Flip Turn` in the trace), while the request was made before it. The engine's value at the request is the one a player sees. The fix belongs in the spectator's point placement (the lead's lane, python/duoforge_replay, not edited here); until then `duoforge.python.replay` stays red on this battle.
   - **Campaign.** `tools/cloud/aws_fuzz/campaigns/g51-mega4` (teams D: the holders of Chandelure, Houndoom, Lucario, Dragalge, Crabominable and Chimecho; E: Glalie, Pinsir, Banette, Pidgeot with Pidgeotite (Keen Eye), Incineroar and Milotic; F: Pidgeot with Big Pecks, Garchomp, Gholdengo, Pelipper, Raichu and Sneasler; fifteen pairings among them and with teams A and B, seed 9751001, 300 battles): **PASS 300**, DIVERGENCE 0, UNSUPPORTED 0, ORACLE_GAP 0, REF_ERROR 0, CAP 0; the domain check agreed in 961 of 961 sampled requests.

5at. **G54, moves batch** (no public value beyond `DUOFORGE_BLOCK_QUICK_GUARD` = 6, which decision 0029 approves; one internal draw site `DFI_SITE_MULTIHIT_COUNT` = 21 in `src/rng/draw.h` (site 20 is the G46 drag); handler ids 71 to 76 and `UNMODELED` 77 in the pool tables; no state beyond the tail rev 4 fields, and the rev 5 layout of 5aq is unchanged). Marked:
   - **Icicle Spear and Scale Shot** (`DFI_SPECIAL_MULTI_HIT_2_5`, `DFI_SPECIAL_SCALE_SHOT`): a hit count of 2 to 5 from the Champions sample `[2 x7, 3 x7, 4 x3, 5 x3]` (`data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:440`, the same list as `sim/battle-actions.ts:870`), kept in `src/combat/multihit_count.h` and tested index by index in `duoforge.state.pool_g54`; one draw of random(20) only when a target survived the hit steps. Scale Shot lowers the user's Defense by 1 and raises its Speed by 1 after the last hit.
   - **Quick Guard** (`DFI_SPECIAL_QUICK_GUARD`): a protect move of positive priority stops at a guarded side with `-activate move: Quick Guard` (BLOCKED detail 6); the side flag is cleared in the residual and reported as `guard_flags` bit 2, with view feature 38 supported.
   - **Upper Hand** (`DFI_SPECIAL_UPPER_HAND`): fails unless the target queued a priority move that is not Status (a queue read); a target with Prankster or Gale Wings is refused.
   - **Heal Pulse** (`DFI_SPECIAL_HEAL_PULSE`): dispatched after the Protect, TryHit and accuracy steps; a target that Protect stopped shows only the Protect line and no heal; a target at full HP fails with a plain `-fail` (no `[still]`); otherwise it heals by half of its maximum HP.
   - **Strength Sap** (`DFI_SPECIAL_STRENGTH_SAP`): dispatched after the accuracy draw (accuracy 100); a target at Attack -6 fails with `[still]` after that draw (reference: `g54_strength_sap_b` step 7, ACCURACY then `-fail`); otherwise the user heals by the target's Attack and the target's Attack drops by one stage.
   - **Sing** is a data row (sleep, accuracy 55).
   - **Dropped, with the engine gap of each** (decision: the pin lines, not the tracker, decide): **Haze** (`data/moves.ts:8156-8172`: `onHitField` prints `-clearallboost` and clears every active's boosts). The engine has no clear-all-boosts event (`include/duoforge/duoforge.h`, the boost events are 16 and 17 only), and `python/duoforge_live/lines.py:84` lists `-clearallboost` in UNREPRESENTABLE_KINDS, so the live adapter cannot hold it either: a new public event kind is needed. **Steel Beam** (`data/moves.ts:17876-17892`: `mindBlownRecoil` and `onMoveFail` self-damage of `Math.round(maxhp / 2)` under the move's own condition; `sim/battle-actions.ts:1380-1393`, the recoil on a hit with that condition as its effect). The engine's recoil path (`src/combat/turn.c`, the recoil block near the Struggle recoil) handles Struggle and fractional recoil only, the cause list (`duoforge.h` 1041-1071) has RECOIL but no move-named cause, and a miss has no self-damage branch. **Final Gambit** (`data/moves.ts:5301-5322`: damage equal to the user's current HP, then `pokemon.faint()`, `selfdestruct: "ifHit"`; `sim/pokemon.ts:1581-1592` queues the faint). The engine has no fixed damage equal to the user's HP in `dfi_get_damage`, and no self-KO move: Explosion and Self-Destruct are unmarked (`DFI_MOVE_EXPLOSION` 195, `DFI_MOVE_SELFDESTRUCT` 395), so this is a rule to add, not a public value.
   - Mega Launcher, Skill Link and Loaded Dice stay unmarked (the pulse guard, the maximum hit count and the hit-count rule are not modelled).
   - **Bugs found and fixed in this step**: Strength Sap's `-6` fail is after its accuracy draw (the first version had it before, which the reference trace contradicts); Heal Pulse is dispatched after Protect (the first version ignored Protect).
   - **Evidence.** 18 G54 battles under `"data": "pool"` (`g54_*`); `duoforge.state.pool_g54` (81 checks, including the 2-5 table index by index and the Quick Guard flag); pool conformance 395 battles (5538 checks, 0 failures, with main's G49 battles); tiebreak pool data 395 battles; the full local CTest run 756 of 756 on the merged tree. Random campaign (`diff_driver.py random`, seed 5401, 300 battles, pairings DE, ED, DD, EE with two G54 teams): 300 PASS, 0 DIVERGENCE, 0 UNSUPPORTED, 0 ORACLE_GAP, 0 REF_ERROR, 0 CAP, before and after the merge.
   - **Mutation checks**: 13 of 14 caught (`scratchpad/mutate_g54.py`, one edit each in `turn.c`, `multihit_count.h`, `observation.c` or `support_manifest.c`). M12 (Upper Hand reads a Status move queued by its target as priority) survived the first run. It is closed by the battle `g54_upper_hand_followme`: Upper Hand (+3) acts before a target queuing Follow Me (Status, +2), so the queue read must refuse it, and the trace shows the `-fail`; the mutant would hit Indeedee there. Protect (+4) always acts before Upper Hand, so the Protect case never reaches the Status check; `g54_upper_hand_protect` covers the non-Ghost fail. The mutation run itself was not repeated after this change.

5au. **G56, Part 1: the TryMove order audit (no engine change; citations of the pin).** The pinned `useMoveInner` (`sim/battle-actions.ts:467-523`) takes the targets from `getMoveTargets` (467), runs `TryMove` (`singleEvent` 486, `runEvent` 488) and returns on a failure; only then does the no-target path run (`!targets.length` at 510-514: `[notarget]`, and `-fail` for gen 5 and later; the missing-target branch at 461-465 is before `getMoveTargets`). `Try` (the `onTry` gate) runs in `trySpreadMoveHit` (`sim/battle-actions.ts:590`) and in `tryMoveHit` (826), and `TryHit` (`onTryHit`) in `trySpreadMoveHit` (644), so both are reached only with targets left. The Champions mod overrides `spreadMoveHit` (`data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:314`), whose `TryHit` runs only with a target. Consequence for the marked moves: the only `onTryMove` gates among them are Electro Shot and Solar Beam (charge moves, handled before the no-target path in the engine: `src/combat/turn.c`, the charge branch before `dfi_no_target`), and Double Shock (its `onTryMove` gate runs before the no-target path; fixed in #277). Every marked `onTry`, `onTryHit` and `onTryMove` gate of the other moves (Fake Out, First Impression, Sucker Punch, Steel Roller, Grass Knot, Low Kick, Helping Hand, Follow Me, Wide Guard, Aurora Veil, Rage Powder, Psychic Fangs, Yawn, Disable, Trick, Switcheroo, Clangorous Soul, Brick Break) runs after the no-target path, as the engine has it. The Armor Tail check is the one shared `TryMove`-class gate on main, before `dfi_no_target` (`turn.c`). A committed battle for a no-target `onTry` move was looked for (a plan search of 2000 trials for Sucker Punch with `[notarget]`) and not found, so the audit has no regression battle yet.

5aw. **G56, Part 2: Outrage as a lockedmove (marked; Thrash and Petal Dance are not marked).** The pin (`data/conditions.ts:253-285`): `lockedmove` has duration 2; its `onStart` draws `trueDuration = random(2, 4)` (2 or 3); its `onResidual` counts `trueDuration` down every residual and deletes the volatile silently when the holder is asleep; its `onRestart` keeps duration 2 only while `trueDuration >= 2` (a successful hit by the self effect); its `onAfterMove` removes the volatile at duration 1, after the use whatever its result; its `onEnd` adds confusion only when `trueDuration <= 1`. Engine representation (tail rev 4, `lock_turns` = `trueDuration`, 0 when there is no lock; the slot's `locked_move` = the move slot + 1, the body's half of the pairing of section 7): the count draw is `DFI_SITE_LOCK_TURNS` (2 to 4, site 21, the converter's `LOCK_TURNS`), and the count goes down at the use, not in the residual: a restarting use decrements it in its AfterMove, so a replacement's pause (an Eject Button, a pivot) cannot strand the restart in a call that does not have it. A use that does not restart the lock ends it there (`dfi_lock_end`: confusion at `trueDuration <= 1`). The request offers the locked move alone, with no target; the lock is cleared silently by a switch-out, a faint and a sleep at the residual; the locked move uses no PP. Refused with `E_UNSUPPORTED`, decided before anything changes: a locked user whose move is stopped before its use (a flinch, a freeze, a full paralysis; a sleep is not refused, its lock is deleted at the residual), and a White Herb holder with a stat down when a lock ends at this use (the herb's AnyAfterMove and the confusion would both print; their order is not modelled). Thrash and Petal Dance share the handler (`LOCKED_MOVE`) but stay unmarked: no recorded battle yet. Evidence: seven recorded battles under POOL (`g56_*`): `g56_outrage_protect` (Protect blocks the first use: no lock), `g56_outrage_full` (a full lock of three uses that ends in confusion), `g56_outrage_cut` (a lock cut by a Protect with no confusion, its count above one), `g56_outrage_disrupt` (cut by a Protect, then confusion), `g56_outrage_confusion` (a Protect-blocked locked use ends in confusion, the battle has an Eject Button replacement during the lock), `g56_outrage_switch` (a lock dragged out by Roar, cleared with no confusion) and `g56_outrage_faint` (a faint on the turn the lock would start, from Rocky Helmet). Gap, stated: no battle faints a locked user during an active lock (the foe's Sucker Punch never KOs the lead in the roughly 1,000 trials of the searches); the refusal paths above are unit-tested (`duoforge.state.pool_g56`), not replayed. Random campaign (seed 56, 300 battles over four pairings of the two prepared teams, Showdown as the judge): PASS 297, UNSUPPORTED 3 (explicit engine refusals), ORACLE_GAP 0, no DIVERGENCE. The ties of a lockedmove with the silent ends of stall and Protect (59 in the campaign, PASS) are dropped by the converter's named rule `lock_counter_tie` (`tools/reference/trace_to_c.py`): the reference shuffles them, but neither order shows in a line, a draw or a state that the other reads, and the engine's list keeps the lock at the pin's group (no order, sub-order 2, as every condition). The three UNSUPPORTED battles are one shape: a confused lock user whose confusion self-hit stops its move, so the lock reaches the residual unused (the pin ends it there, `fieldEvent` at duration 1); the engine cannot tell a used lock from an unused one at the residual (the count goes down at the use), so it refuses that step (E_UNSUPPORTED, `pool_g56` covers it with a seeded roll). Mutation check: ten anchored mutants of the engine, with the G56 tests as the oracle; all ten are caught, the last (a sleeping holder keeps its lock into the residual) by the case of `duoforge.state.pool_g56` added for it.
6. **G3, the POOL state tail** (section 7): the container for the state of G7 to G11, under the POOL kinds only, as its own schema (0x0103; revision 2, schema 0x0203, adds the state that the view fields of decision 0018 need: section 7). No mechanic and no public field; the CLOSURE and TEAM_C states are byte for byte unchanged.

7. **The whole legal pool as rows** (roadmap M11: learning from all Showdown replays; owner request of 2026-10-02): DuoForge must **encode** every team of the format, not simulate it, so a row for every forme, move, item and ability of the legal pool is added to the POOL tables (section 4.2). Every new row is unmarked; nothing about the engine changes.

5av. **G52, Revival Blessing** (decision 0025 item 6 and the revive part of items 8 to 10; `data/moves.ts:15110-15136`, `sim/side.ts` 925-985 and 1119-1132, `sim/battle.ts` 2781-2797, 2862-2891). One handler id, `REVIVAL_BLESSING` (72, after Double Shock's 71; UNMODELED moves to 73); the move is marked. `onTryHit` fails with a plain `-fail` and `[still]` (the no-target line) when the user's side has no fainted brought member. Otherwise the user's slot is flagged (switch flag 7, `DFI_SWITCH_REVIVE_BLESSING`) and a PIVOT is made with no check of a living reserve: the revive names a brought fainted member (a fainted active ally too: its instaswitch). A pass is offered when the flagged slots exceed the living reserves, and a revive takes one of min(flagged, living reserves) forced switches while it is accepted regardless (`chooseSwitch`'s Revival Blessing branch). The revive is `sethp(maxhp / 2)`, status cleared, the REVIVE event (public value 43, PERCENT for the foe, EXACT for the owner). The revive's pivot is answered in the step after the move line; the converter keeps each side's Revival Blessing user across steps. **Not in this step**: decision 0023's revived foe HP (the world builder's uniform draw over the public percent is not the revived value; open: the refusal the coordinator proposed (a public record with a foe REVIVE is E_UNSUPPORTED) needs the revive to be a public fact the record can hold; the record keeps no revive history and the knowledge of a revived member looks like any heal, so it needs a public marker, a layout change, which is not in this PR), and the Regenerator instaswitch, which is refused with E_UNSUPPORTED (no recorded battle reaches it). Encoder 5 (decision 0028 and 0026): the REVIVE slot row and the ILLUSION_UP rule in `encode.c` (bit 19 for the own side only).
   - **Harness** (tools/reference, tools/difftest, not the engine): the random player offers fainted members for a revive, the fainted active ally included, and keeps a forced switch within the reserves; a trial keeps the switch flags (a revive clears its Pokemon's flag, which left later trials on a changed request: the domain samples lost choices); the record reader takes the REVIVE slot command (kind 4).
   - **Engine findings from the probes**: a pass keeps the Revival Blessing flag (sim/side.ts choosePass; the side is asked again while a fainted member stands); the forced choice is read slot by slot (a normal switch takes a unit, a revive takes a unit if one is left, a pass takes a forced pass, the choice ends with no unit left); the pivot is answered as the reference's `runAction` asks (`dfi_pivot_pending` after each action).
   - **Mutation checks**: 13 mutants of the revive rules (`g52_mutate.py`, one edit each in `turn.c` or `request.c`, each rebuilt and run against `duoforge.state.pool_g52`, `pool_setup`, `pool_tail`, `reference.conformance_pool_data`, `request.domain` and `encode`). Caught 9: the revive that never fails, the plain `-fail`, the flag never set, the HP a third, the pivot answer not queued, the pass dropped, the forced count ignoring the revive slots, a revive that takes no unit, the pivot skipped on the last action. Survived 4: two equivalent in reachable states (`dfi_side_can_answer` without the fainted check: a flag exists only while a fainted member stands; the revived status clear: a fainted Pokemon has no status), and two gaps: **the instaswitch** (no recorded battle reaches it, so R07 survives) and **the living revive candidate** (the C unit checks do not name a living member; the reference's domain samples would refuse it, so R12 is a gap in the C tests only).
   - **Review round 2 (the failure line)**: the reference line is `|move|p1a: Pawmot|Revival Blessing||[still]` followed by `|-fail|p1a: Pawmot` (`tests/reference/traces/g52_fail_no_fainted_pawmot.json`, step 1, line 851). The plain `-fail` of the first engine draft was wrong; the no-target line is pinned by `duoforge.reference.conformance_pool_data` (that battle), which killed the plain-`-fail` mutant R02. **The living revive candidate** has a white-box check in `duoforge.state.pool_setup` (one fainted and one standing reserve: exactly one revive candidate, the fainted one), which kills R12. **Mutants after round 2: 11 of 13 caught**; R04 and R06 remain equivalent in reachable states.
   - **Recordings**: five POOL battles in `tests/reference/specs` and `traces` (`g52_*`, from the kept random campaigns 5401 and 5402, the purposes name the species and its gender): a benched Kingambit (M) revived at a pivot with a standing reserve, the failed revive at turn 1 (Pawmot, M), a benched Primarina (F) revived and later switched in by the player, Milotic (F) revived in a won battle, and a Ceruledge (M) revive at a pivot with no living reserve. Each reproduces its trace (`ps_trace --check`). **Instaswitch** (review round 2): `g52_instaswitch_faint_previous_turn` (Milotic, F, faints at step 14 with no living reserve; Revival Blessing at step 15; the revive into the empty slot at step 16) and `g52_instaswitch_faint_earlier_slot_empty` (the same battle, the slot empty for three steps, Pawmot's Revival Blessing at step 17, the revive at step 18).
