Both are explicit-choice specs: the first is random play of campaign 5403 (seed 5403, battle 34) taken as it was, the second the same choices with Pawmot's move 1 at steps 15 and 16 (so the slot stays empty), written by editing the choices, not by hand from scratch.
   - **Campaigns** (`diff_driver.py random`, pairings XY, YX, XX, YY over the G52 team X (Pawmot with Revival Blessing, Primarina, Kingambit, Ceruledge, Milotic, Gholdengo) and the g50 team Y, data POOL, domain rate 0.1, harness 14, pin b2cb775): seed 5401, 300 battles, PASS 300, DIVERGENCE 0, REF_ERROR 0, 1744 sampled requests all accepted, none dropped; seed 5402 (the revive policy: half of the revive slots without a living reserve revive), 300 battles, PASS 299, UNSUPPORTED 1 (`fz_5402_172`: a burn faint in a residual tie, the existing refusal `dfi_residual_order_ambiguous`), no divergence; seed 5403 (the merged tree with origin/main), 300 battles, PASS 300, DIVERGENCE 0, REF_ERROR 0, UNSUPPORTED 0, 1855 sampled requests all accepted, none dropped. The earlier probe at seed 5203 (60 battles) went from 7 divergences to 0 with the engine fixes above.
   - **Tables** (after the merge with main's G45, #280): `gen_closure.py --pool` regenerated (205 UNMODELED moves, 129 UNMODELED abilities; canonical sha256 `15ba2c499e28c80385363a07e7030305dfbb62d08cf6d3c919e2fbaa9ded6037`; the model's POOL fingerprints KP `12e1e714f3433ec05aac835ec403853fd47985abbb5c7cf669b4583957d677c8`, KPD `32e43051af25e2f0768dfa836f013d7f0b9439053aa9b9e1ef69addff0bb35c6`); the conformance pool has 427 battles and 2501 tiebreak stops.
   - **Version**: no bump in this PR (the library version stays what main has).
5bb. **G47, the status and volatile cures: Synchronize, Oblivious, Lum Berry and Mental Herb; a fix of the G36 Toxic row** (no public value, no new state, no new draw site, no view change, no version bump; the Champions mod overrides none of the four). Marked: the abilities Synchronize (`data/abilities.ts:4857-4871`) and Oblivious (`data/abilities.ts:3008-3040`) and the items Lum Berry (`data/items.ts:3537-3560`) and Mental Herb (`data/items.ts:3889-3926`); all four are engine rows read by id, with the status origins of `dfi_try_status` (OTHER, MOVE, SYNC, HAZARD: what the effect that sets a status is to its messages, `src/combat/turn.c`). **Synchronize** passes a psn, tox, brn or par that another Pokemon gave the holder back to that Pokemon, after `-activate|holder|ability: Synchronize`, through its trySetStatus with the Synchronize effect: the source's immunities and fail lines are those of a move's own status, Flower Veil's block stays silent for it (`data/abilities.ts:1438`), its start line has no `[from]`, and a source that already has a status fails. Not for slp or frz, not from Toxic Spikes, not to the holder itself; the source's own Synchronize answers in turn. The paths that set a status from a source all go through it: move statuses, secondaries, Dire Claw's pick, Poison Touch, Flame Body, Static. **Lum Berry**: eaten at any status set on the holder (AfterSetStatus, priority -1, after Synchronize's 0) and at Update when the holder has a status or is confused (`-enditem|X|Lum Berry|[eat]`, then the cure with `[msg]` and the end of the confusion), refused by a foe's Unnerve (TryEatItem). The confusion half is reached through Hurricane (marked; its 30 percent secondary is a confusion). **Mental Herb**: at Update, used when its holder has a taunt, an encore, a disable or a heal block, and then those are removed in the pin's order (taunt, encore, disable, heal block); attract and torment are not modelled (no volatile of either exists). Encore on a holder of Mental Herb keeps the target's queued move (Champions `encore`, `data/mods/champions/moves.ts:330`), replacing the former E_UNSUPPORTED. **Oblivious**: Taunt is -immune to its holder; Intimidate's Attack drop is -fail with `[from] ability: Oblivious`; a Trace copy of Oblivious onto a taunted Pokemon is refused (E_UNSUPPORTED; the copy comes at entry, where no taunt stands). Its Attract and Captivate halves are not reached (unmarked moves, no Attract volatile). Breakable: no Mold Breaker is marked. **The G36 Toxic row, fixed in this step**: a tox status now resets the toxic stage when it starts (`tox` onStart, `data/conditions.ts:141-150`), and a Lum Berry cure ends the stage with the status (the state invariant allows a stage only on a badly poisoned Pokemon). The switch-in order of two entering badly poisoned Pokemon of one Speed draws the tie: the tox status has an onSwitchIn handler (`data/conditions.ts:151`, the stage back to 0), which `dfi_has_switch_in` now counts. The converter's switch-order rule already counted the reference's own handler total, so it needed no change; it and the engine now agree. The gap predates G47 (the tox status is G36's) and was found by the campaign below (seed 9800000, battle 96 of pairing DD). Numbers changed: unmodelled items 34 to 32 (then 29 with main's G49), unmodelled abilities 137 to 135; the POOL table hash is `3205ab99...` (`gen_closure.py --pool --check`), the model's `POOL_TABLE_HASH` (`tools/state_model/state_v3_model.py`) carries the same value, the POOL fingerprints are those of the model (`FP_KP` 9e89c7ff..., `FP_KPD` 031689ae...), and the pool-data conformance count is 397. **Evidence**: twenty recorded POOL battles: `g47_synchronize_*` (nine, and `g47_synchronize_flower_veil`), `g47_lum_berry`, `g47_lum_unnerve`, `g47_lum_confusion`, `g47_herb_taunt`, `g47_herb_encore`, `g47_herb_disable`, `g47_herb_heal_block`, `g47_oblivious`, `g47_switch_tox_tie` (a double replacement after two faints: the two poisoned Houndoom enter in one switch-in sort), `g47_toxic_reset` (the Lum Berry cure, then the poison starts again); `tests/test_pool_g47.c` (the marks, and the state after each rule's step, including the toxic stage 1 after the re-poison); pool-data conformance; `checkG47` (stub calls of the pinned handlers). **Mutants**, one each in `src/combat/turn.c`, rebuilt: round one (six): Synchronize's rule dropped, Lum Berry's Unnerve refusal dropped, Encore's Mental Herb guard dropped, Oblivious's Taunt branch dropped, Synchronize's origin read as a move's (caught by conformance only), Lum Berry's confusion cure dropped; all six caught by at least one suite. Round two (five): Flower Veil's silence for Synchronize (caught by conformance; the G47 state test sees no line), the toxic stage reset at a status start (survives both suites: an equivalent mutant, since the stage is zero whenever the status is not tox, by the invariant and the cure clear), the toxic stage clear on a Lum Berry cure (caught by both: the invariant fails), the switch-in toxic term (caught by both), and Mental Herb's removal order of taunt and heal block (survives both: no battle puts two of the four volatiles on one holder before an Update, so the order is not observed). Round two caught 3 of 5, the two survivors are explained above. **Campaign** (`tools/cloud/aws_fuzz/campaigns/g47-cures`, 300 random battles, seed 9800000, 15 pairings): before the fix 299 PASS and 1 DIVERGENCE (`fz_9800000_96`); after the fix 300 PASS. **Python**: `duoforge.python.replay` and `duoforge.python.live` pass on the merged tree (main's #274 is in). **Open**: none in this step.

5ay. **G60, Substitute** (decision 0032). The values approved by HauptSession: `DUOFORGE_VOLATILE_SUBSTITUTE` 9, `DUOFORGE_FAIL_SUBSTITUTE_EXISTS` 1 and `DUOFORGE_FAIL_SUBSTITUTE_WEAK` 2 (valid only with cause MOVE and id2 Substitute), the public cause `DUOFORGE_PUBLIC_CAUSE_SUBSTITUTE` 8, and the generated 0/1 column `dfi_pool_move_bypasssub` (flags.bypasssub, appended last to the canonical pool bytes, as lane B's reflectable column). No tail byte: `substitute_hp` exists since rev 2 (section 7). Marked: the move Substitute (`data/moves.ts:18305-18374`: the fails, the cost floor(maxhp/4), the start, the gate of each hit that a target does not take, the break), and the marked rows that a Substitute changes: Intimidate (`data/abilities.ts:2201`, `-immune` for a Substitute, no drop), Infiltrator (the user's moves pass the Substitute, `data/abilities.ts:2133`), the nine resist berries (no eating under a hit that the Substitute takes, `data/items.ts:1030-1046` and siblings), and the bypasssub moves (through the column). Refused: Baton Pass, Shed Tail, Tidy Up, Defog, Aromatherapy, Sky Drop, Sparkly Swirl, Disguise, Ice Face, Supersweet Syrup; an OHKO break is not represented (no marked OHKO row). The public record and the honest world refuse any Substitute on either side with cause 8. Evidence: eight recorded POOL battles with genders stated and six members a side (`tests/reference/specs/g60_sub_*.json`): the break of a Substitute by a contact move with Rough Skin on the attacker (`g60_sub_basic`), an absorbed hit and a second Substitute that fails with EXISTS (`g60_sub_absorb`), a status move that fails on the user against a Substitute and a Substitute that the user cannot re-raise under Taunt (`g60_sub_yawn`, `g60_sub_taunt`: Taunt passes the Substitute by its bypasssub flag), a multi-hit move whose hits are absorbed while the loop goes on (`g60_sub_scale_shot`), Close Combat breaking a Substitute with the Chople Berry not eaten and its self drops (`g60_sub_chople`), Intimidate's `-immune` on a Substitute beside a drop (`g60_sub_intimidate`), and an Infiltrator's Quick Attack passing a Substitute (`g60_sub_infiltrator`). Tests: `duoforge.state.pool_g60` (the values, the marks, the bypasssub column, the public refusal with cause 8 on a replayed battle), and the conformance of all 493 pool battles (`duoforge.reference.conformance_pool_data`, 7000 checks, 0 failures; closure and Team C unchanged). Mutations (the rule edited, the tests that must fail): the cost off by one (caught by the conformance), the bypasssub column ignored (caught), the Infiltrator bypass removed (caught), the status gate off (caught), the berry eaten under a Substitute (caught), Intimidate ignoring a Substitute (caught), the self-drop roll ignoring absorbed targets (caught), the multi-hit target not restored (caught), the break without its -end event (caught), the cause bit off (caught by `duoforge.state.pool_g60`), the weak check at the boundary `<` for `<=` (caught by `g60_sub_weak`: Gengar at 41 of 164, `4 * hp == maxhp`, fails with [weak]), the drain rounding of the Substitute's drain (caught by `g60_sub_drain`: the break heals ceil(51 / 2) = 26), and the presence writer off (caught by `duoforge.state.pool_g60`, 4 failures). Eleven of eleven rule mutants are caught. Known gap: the Infiltrator and drain paths are one recorded battle each (the kill is by one battle). The foe-side Substitute in the live tracker is not folded (decision 0032 section 8, by design). Campaign: 300 random battles of seed 60 (teams D, E, F, six pairings), PASS 300, 1682 requests sampled, none dropped (`diff_driver.py random`, 2 workers, the machine lock taken per chunk).

### 4.1 Data query API (names and legality)


One query API answers what a caller needs to build a setup that a context accepts (`duoforge_data_count`, `_name`, `_find`, `_supported`, `_forme_info`, `_forme_moves`; public header, section "data query").

- **Same rule, one implementation.** The legality of a move or ability for a forme, the legal genders, the Mega forme and its stone, and the support gate by id are functions of `closure_member.c`. Setup, the member invariant and the API all call them; the API holds no copy of a rule.
- **Names.** `gen_closure.py --pool` writes the Showdown id (toID) of every forme (the Mega formes included), move, item, ability and nature into `pool_tables.c` in the same run as the tables, as designated initializers, so a name cannot drift from its id. They are in no canonical bytes, table hash or fingerprint. A kind reads its prefix; an id at or beyond the kind's count is `E_INVALID_ARGUMENT` for every call, so a POOL move under TEAM_C is refused.
- **Ids.** An id is the table row. A member's ability and item fields are 1 + the id (0 is none), as at setup.
- **Struggle.** It is a row of the move table. `find` returns it, no forme lists it (so it is legal for no member), and the manifest leaves it unmarked, so `duoforge_data_supported` says false for it.
- **Support.** `supported` is the gate of `duoforge_battle_create` by id: the manifest mark, and nothing while the turn core is not marked. A member is supported exactly when its ability, item and moves are and, if it holds its forme's stone, Mega Evolution into the Mega forme is (`mega_supported` of the forme info). A test checks that composition against the white-box gate over random manifests.
- **Evidence.** `duoforge.data.api` builds random members and sides, seeded, only from what the API reports under all six combat kinds, and requires setup to accept them (or to return `E_UNSUPPORTED` exactly when the API says so); mutations to something the API excludes, and an exhaustive sweep of formes against move, ability, gender, item, nature and species ids, must be `E_INVALID_ARGUMENT`. The names are checked against the headers' macros and `trace_to_c.key` (`duoforge.data.pool_names`) and against the pinned dex (`duoforge.data.pool_families`).

### 4.2 The whole legal pool (step 7)

Section 8 rejected generating the whole pool once, because the row format had to grow first and 510 moves would have been encoded before any was tested. The owner now asks for it, with a rule that keeps the first objection: a row is only data, and a row the tables do not fully model says so.

- **Rows.** Every legal forme (264 rows for the 293 selectable formes, the 29 cosmetic copies being **name aliases** in `dfi_pool_forme_aliases`, plus the 82 Mega formes), move (510 and Struggle), item (166) and ability (215) of `docs/research/expansion/data/legal_pool.json`, re-validated at the pin, after the rows of P1 and G2. The closure, Team C and G2 rows are unchanged (the prefix test compares the closure and Team C rows with the extended tables and pins the hash of the closure-layout bytes of the 28 formes, 72 moves, 52 items and 29 abilities of the steps).
- **UNMODELED.** A row that has any callback, any field, target class or flag that the tables do not model gets the handler `UNMODELED` (the special column of a move, a handler column for items and abilities) and the list of those features, as a generated comment and as data (`dfi_pool_*_unmodeled`). The generator still fails for what it cannot read (a type, category, target class, PP or flag it does not know; a value that does not fit its byte). The rules are in `tools/datagen/README.md`.
- **Never marked.** Every new row is unmarked, modelled or not. A test fails for a marked row with the UNMODELED handler or a feature list (`duoforge.data.pool_tables`); a step that implements a row makes it modelled in the generator first (a handler id of its own for a move, `ENGINE_ROWS` for an item or ability, `ENGINE_PIVOT_MOVES` for a pivot), then marks it with its recorded battles.
- **Flags.** The move flags byte is not widened. A flag that no bit holds matters only when a modelled row reads it, and then the move rows that carry it are UNMODELED with the flag in their list.
- **Formes.** Types, base stats, abilities and legal abilities, the Champions learnsets (as P1b), the gender rule and the Mega links (stone, Mega forme, Mega ability). A base forme links the first Mega forme of its stones; the second Mega forme of Absol, Charizard, Garchomp, Lucario and Raichu is a row of its own that the engine cannot reach (the data query API says it is unsupported).
- **Widths.** The pool has 346 formes and 511 moves, more than a u8 holds, so the **pool rows have their own types with u16 forme links** (the closure and extended rows and their bytes are unchanged) and the pool canonical bytes are a new layout, so the POOL fingerprint changes (it is expected to change with every pool row change). Ids are u16 in the state, the observation and the events (a member's species and each move slot), an item or ability is 1 + the id in a byte (an id is at most 254); the generator reads these widths from the sources and `closure_member.c` asserts them at build time.
- **Names and the data API.** The names are generated as for every other row (section 4.1) and the API answers for all rows; aliases are data (`dfi_pool_forme_aliases`); by the owner's decision `duoforge_data_find` also accepts the 29 aliases of the SPECIES table and maps each to its canonical row (bound by the kind's count like the row), while `duoforge_data_name` keeps returning the canonical name; the header contract says so and the next minor version is assigned at merge. The differential test of the API (`duoforge.data.api`) runs over the whole pool: every row of every table is in a setup that the oracle written from the API's answers judges, and setup returns `E_UNSUPPORTED` exactly when the API says so.
- **Evidence.** `duoforge.data.pool_tables` (counts, prefix, hashes, layout, handler columns, no marked half-modelled row), `duoforge.data.pool_rows` (the tables against the validator's output, no checkout), `duoforge.data.gen_closure_refusals`, `duoforge.data.pool_families` (the pinned entries and the validator, 191 thousand probes), `duoforge.data.api`, `duoforge.state.pool_setup`. No battle changes: every conformance header and the tiebreak fixture are byte for byte as before.

## 5. Evidence for every step

- Unit tests with expectations derived from the reference, plus negative cases (the gate before marking; a POOL id under CLOSURE or TEAM_C is `E_INVALID_ARGUMENT` at setup and `E_INVARIANT` in a decoded state).
- Reference battles: spec, then `tools/reference/ps_trace.js` from the committed path, then `tools/reference/trace_to_c.py`, under the POOL kinds.
- A differential replay of all committed battles.
- A random campaign over teams that carry the new members. This needs teams beyond A, B and C: `diff_random.py` takes team files, and later the team generator (component 5).
- The full local CI.

## 6. Corpus and pause file (the differential loop, Builder A)

- **Corpus.** `tests/reference/corpus/` holds specs and `*.json.gz` traces.
  - A CTest replays them through the converter and the C runner without Node, so they run in every CI job, sanitizers included.
  - A battle is promoted when it found a defect (minimised to the shortest prefix that still shows it) or adds a protocol line, draw site or request situation that no committed battle has.
  - A test enforces the cap of about 20 MB.
- **Pause file.**
  - The path comes from `DUOFORGE_FUZZ_PAUSE`, by default `$TEMP/duoforge-fuzz.pause`.
  - `diff_random.py` checks it before each chunk and waits, with a log line, while it exists.
  - Measurements create it and remove it afterwards. `docs/TESTING_AND_BENCHMARKS.md` documents it.

## 7. The POOL state tail (step G3)

The owner decided (2026-10-02): the pool mechanics of steps G7 to G11 (Wide Guard, Throat Chop and Heal Block, Encore, Soak) need state that the schema-3 layout has no room for. They get a **POOL-only tail**, not schema 4.

- **Registry.** The state artifact (kind BATTLE_STATE, semantics 3) has two schemas, told apart by the envelope's `schema_version` (u16; the low byte is the layout of the body, the high byte the revision of the tail):

  | Schema | Name | Size | Carried by |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 3 (0x0003) | v3 | 1009 | CLOSURE, CLOSURE_DEV, TEAM_C, TEAM_C_DEV, SYNTHETIC: unchanged, byte for byte |
  | 0x0403 | v3 + pool tail rev 4 | 1297 | POOL, POOL_DEV |
  | 0x0303 | v3 + pool tail rev 3 | 1257 | none: **refused** by name (`E_SCHEMA_MISMATCH`, tested by artifact and by schema list); rev 4 is 40 bytes longer and moves the members and the positions' offsets inside every side, so a rev 3 artifact cannot decode as a rev 4 one: there is no migration |
  | 0x0203 | v3 + pool tail rev 2 | 1257 | none: **refused** by every decoder (`E_SCHEMA_MISMATCH`); rev 3 has the same size and gives the byte at +26 of a position (reserved in rev 2) a meaning, so a rev 2 artifact would decode as a rev 3 one if it were accepted: there is no migration |
  | 0x0103 | v3 + pool tail rev 1 | 1051 | none: **refused** by every decoder (`E_SCHEMA_MISMATCH`, like any unknown schema); there is no migration, because pool states are not frozen (no certificate, no committed state artifact of a POOL kind) |
  | 4 | (reserved) | - | the certified pool teams, see below |

  The context artifact and the semantics id are unchanged, so no fingerprint changes (the POOL fingerprint does not depend on the state layout). The schema is a function of the context's kind: a POOL context decodes only 0x0403 and every other kind only 3.
- **Layout (rev 4, cut B of `docs/research/expansion/tail-rev4-proposal.md`; rev 3 is the same without the fields marked rev 4 and with positions of 32 bytes, members of 8 and sides of 120).** The 1009 bytes of schema 3, then **288 bytes** (`src/codec/state_codec.h` has the offsets and static asserts; `tools/state_model/state_v3_model.py` is the oracle, `--pool-tail`): an 8-byte field block, then two sides of 140 bytes.

  | Block | Offset | Bytes |
  |---|---|---|
  | field | 0 | `gravity_turns`, 7 reserved |
  | side s | 8 + 140 s | `wide_guard`, `aurora_veil_turns`, `toxic_spikes`, `stealth_rock`, `spikes`, `sticky_web`, `quick_guard`, `hazard_order` (rev 4) (u8 each), no reserved byte, then 2 positions of 36 bytes (+8), then 6 roster members of 10 bytes (+80) |
  | position p | +8 + 36 p | `last_move`, `encore_slot`, `encore_turns`, `throat_chop_turns`, `heal_block_turns` (rev 1), `perish`, `taunt_turns`, `disable_slot`, `disable_turns`, `imprison`, `must_recharge`, `trap_turns`, `trap_source`, `trap_band`, `leech_seed_source`, `yawn_turns`, `focus_energy`, `stockpile`, `stockpile_def`, `stockpile_spd`, `charge`, `glaive_rush` (u8 each), `substitute_hp` (u16), `trap_move` (u16), `protect_kind` (u8, rev 3), `move_result`, `single_turn`, `hits_taken`, `ability_state`, `lock_turns` (u8 each, rev 4), 4 reserved |
  | member m | +80 + 10 m | `ability_now` (u16), `forme_now` (u16), `soak_type` (rev 1), `item_now`, `toxic_stage`, `type2`, `flags` (u8 each; `type2` and `flags` rev 4), 1 reserved |

  The reserved bytes are the reserve (35 of the 288: 7 + 2 x (2 x 4 + 6); 43 in rev 3, 47 in rev 2): always written as zero, a decoder refuses anything else (`TAIL_RESERVED`), and a later revision may give them a meaning. The position reserve was 6 bytes so that the flags that decision 0018 declares and this revision does not carry (Destiny Bond, Curse, No Retreat, Salt Cure, Dragon Cheer, Rage Powder) fit without a layout change. The size is fixed; in memory the tail is plain structs of 254 bytes in the battle (`dfi_pool_tail`: per position 32, per side 126, plus the field block's gravity byte and one pad byte that keeps the struct free of padding; since rev 4 a position has 32 data bytes and a side 126, both even, so neither has a pad byte any more), with no pointer and no allocation. The battle struct (`struct duoforge_battle`, what `clone` and `copy` move) is 1288 bytes now (GCC, x86-64; the layout is not a contract; it was 1232 with the first tail, and this revision's tail is 44 bytes larger than rev 3's); the struct is the same for every kind, so a CLOSURE or TEAM_C battle carries the 44 more (zero) bytes in memory too, while its encoding is unchanged. **No performance claim is made: a clone or copy moves 44 bytes (3.6 percent of the struct) more and nothing was measured here; the clone benchmark runs on the GCP machine.** The digest and `equal` buffers on the stack are 1297 bytes (were 1257); nothing is allocated.
- **What the fields are (rev 2).** They are the state that the view fields of decision 0018 need, and nothing else; each is zero until the step that implements its mechanic writes it, and the view fills from them in the steps that implement the view. Per position (the occupant's, cleared with it): `perish` (the duration counter of `perishsong`, 0 to 4; the count shown is one less), `taunt_turns` (0 to 4), `disable_slot` (move slot + 1) with `disable_turns` (1 to 5; zero exactly together), `imprison`, `must_recharge`, `focus_energy`, `charge`, `glaive_rush` (flags), `substitute_hp` (0 none, else its HP, at most a quarter of the occupant's maximum HP, rounded down, as it is made), a partial trap (`trap_turns` 0 to 8, `trap_source` the trapper's flat position + 1, `trap_move` the move id + 1 that names it in the lines, `trap_band` the Binding Band flag of the trapper that decides 1/6 or 1/8: all zero together, the band only with a trap), `leech_seed_source` (flat position + 1 of the one that gets the HP), `yawn_turns` (0 to 2), `stockpile` (0 to 3 layers) with `stockpile_def` and `stockpile_spd` (the boosts the layers gave, at most the layers, because a capped boost gives none). A source is never the occupant's own position. Per side: `aurora_veil_turns` 0 to 8 (5, 8 with Light Clay), `toxic_spikes` 0 to 2, `stealth_rock` and `sticky_web` 0 or 1, `spikes` 0 to 3. Field: `gravity_turns` 0 to 5. Per roster member: `ability_now` (0 = the member's own, else ability id + 1 up to the kind's ability count; Trace, Skill Swap; only for a member standing on the field), `item_now` (0 = as the member says, 1 to the kind's item count = item id + 1, 255 = holds nothing; Trick, Knock Off; outlives the field), `forme_now` (0 = the member's own, else forme id + 1 up to the kind's forme count; Aegislash, Palafin, Mimikyu; outlives the field), `toxic_stage` (0 to 15; only for a member on the field whose status is `DFI_TAIL_TOXIC_STATUS` = 6, `DUOFORGE_AILMENT_TOX`, which no state can have yet because the status bound of every kind is below it: a toxic stage is refused until the step that makes Toxic raises that bound). The bounds are from the pin (`data/moves.ts`, `data/conditions.ts`) and the research, not mechanics: a step that finds one wrong changes the revision (0x0303) and says so.
- **Values (rev 4).** The fields only; no mechanic writes them yet (the steps that need them come after: Stomping Tantrum and Roost, Quick Guard, Rage Fist, Supreme Overlord, Protean and Illusion, Outrage, Burn Up and Double Shock, Zero to Hero), and every one is zero until then. Per position (the occupant's, zero when it leaves or faints, as the position's other fields; the Champions mod also resets `hits_taken` there): `move_result` (two two-bit values, `DFI_MOVE_RESULT_*`: bits 0-1 this turn, bits 2-3 last turn, each 0 undefined, 1 true, 2 false, 3 null; bits 4-7 are zero; Stomping Tantrum reads `=== false`, so null must not count as a failure), `single_turn` (bit 0 `RAGE_POWDER`, bit 1 `ROOST`, bits 2-7 zero; the Rage Powder bit needs the position's Follow Me flag up, which the same move sets and the residual ends with it; Roost's bit has no base flag to belong to), `hits_taken` (0 to 6, saturating; Rage Fist's power is 50 + 50 x n, capped at 350), `ability_state` (0 to 6; its meaning goes with the occupant's current ability: Supreme Overlord's fallen allies 0 to 5, Protean's once-per-switch-in 0/1, Illusion's roster index + 1), `lock_turns` (0 to 3; the turns a `lockedmove` volatile has left: Outrage, Thrash, Petal Dance; step G56). The pairing, as Rage Powder's: a lock needs its locked move in the position's active slot (`locked_move` = the move slot + 1, which the lock sets and clears with it, the body's half of the pairing), so a lock without that move is `TAIL_POSITION`, and a locked move without a charge, a choice lock or a lock is refused by the volatile check (`VOLATILE`). The count goes down at the use (the residual's countdown of the turn, done at the use so that no residual of a later call, after a replacement's pause, has to remember the restart). Per side: `quick_guard` (0 or 1, this turn only; the view already has `guard_flags` bit `QUICK_GUARD`) and `hazard_order` (added to cut B from the reserve by the lead, for step G37: Showdown runs the switch-in handlers of the hazards in the order they were created, the pin's effectOrder, `sim/battle.ts:994-1000`; two bits per slot, slot 0 in bits 1:0 to slot 3 in bits 7:6, each slot a kind: 0 Stealth Rock, 1 Spikes, 2 Toxic Spikes, 3 Sticky Web; with n kinds up (layers above 0) the first n slots are exactly those kinds, each once, and the other slots are 0, so no hazard is the byte 0; a new kind takes slot n, more layers keep their slot, an ended kind leaves and the later ones shift down, a kind put up again goes last; step G37 sets and reads it, this revision has no reader). Per roster member: `type2` (0 none, 1 to 18 = type id + 1, 255 = the type is gone: Burn Up and Double Shock; only for a member on the field, cleared with the occupant and, once a mechanic writes it, by a Mega Evolution like `soak_type`: the Mega clearing comes with that mechanic) and `flags` (bit 0 `HERO_SHOWN`: Zero to Hero's message was shown, once for the battle; bits 1-7 zero). Invariants: `TAIL_POSITION` (a value out of range, a bit that is not defined, Rage Powder's bit without the Follow Me flag), `TAIL_SIDE` (`quick_guard` above 1, a `hazard_order` that is not the permutation above), `TAIL_MEMBER` (`type2` not 0, 1 to 18 or 255, or on a member that is not on the field; `flags` with a bit that is not defined).
- **Values (rev 3).** `protect_kind` of a position: 0 for Protect and Detect, 1 for Spiky Shield (2 would be Baneful Bunker, 3 King's Shield, unused while those moves are unmarked); nonzero only while the position's `DFI_VOL_PROTECT` volatile is up (it ends with the volatile, in the residual; invariant `TAIL_POSITION`), (until rev 4 the pad byte that followed it in memory was zero too; it has no counterpart now).
- **party_order (step G46, rev 4 unchanged in size; bytes +1..+6 of the field block, +7 stays reserved).** The side.pokemon order of the pin, per side, as a permutation of the brought members: leads first, then the bench in pick order. Entry k (k = 0..5, the Showdown index) is roster index + 1 in 3 bits, 0 = empty, packed in 3 bytes per side (entry k at bits 3k..3k+2 of a 24-bit little-endian value; bits 18-23 zero). Written at team selection; every switch-in swaps the incoming entry with the outgoing one's (sim/battle-actions.ts:119-133; a fainted outgoing too; an empty slot changes nothing), so a drag's draw (the bench in this order, sim/battle.ts:1570-1588) and Ally Switch read it, and Illusion will. Invariant `TAIL_PARTY`: the first n entries are the n brought members, each once, the rest empty. POOL kinds only. The reserve count is 29 (35 before). Information: the own side is exact. The foe side is hidden past its two actives: the public record (`duoforge_battle_public`) writes the foe's entries 2..5 as `DFI_PARTY_HIDDEN` (7), as it masks the pick order past the leads, and an honest world (`duoforge_battle_from_view`) takes the foe's party_order from the sampled hypothesis (its brought order, the actives kept at entries 0 and 1, the bench in hypothesis order), never from the true state (decision 0023: "party_order: own side exact, foe side from the hypothesis's brought order"). Not yet read by the engine's drag (step G46 continues).
- **Values (rev 1, unchanged).** `last_move` 0 none, 1 to 4 move slot + 1, 5 Struggle; `encore_slot` 0 or 1 to 4, `encore_turns` 0 to 4, zero exactly together; `throat_chop_turns` 0 to 2; `heal_block_turns` 0 to 5; `wide_guard` 0 or 1; `soak_type` 0 or a type id + 1 (1 to 18).
- **Under POOL the tail is state like any other:** encode, decode, digest, `equal`, `clone`, `copy`, the invariants and the model-facing query check all carry it, and a state of 1009 bytes (or of rev 1's 1051, or of rev 2's and rev 3's 1257) is not a POOL state. A tail never enters an observation, a view or an event: this revision adds no state-side public field, size or constant (`DUOFORGE_STATE_V3_ENCODED_SIZE` stays 1009 and means schema 3; a caller sizes its buffer with `duoforge_battle_encoded_size`, which says 1297 under the POOL kinds). The view values that rev 4 prepares (`DUOFORGE_POSITION_EXT_ROOST`, `DUOFORGE_VIEWEXT_FEATURE_ROOST` 40 and `MOVE_FAILED` 41, `duoforge_position_ext.move_failed`; decision 0018 sections 3.4, 6.1 and 7.1) are a separate commit and set no supported bit.
- **Under the other kinds it is absent:** all zero in memory (invariant `TAIL_KIND`, so check, encode and digest return `E_INVARIANT` for a state that has one) and not in the encoding. The digests of states of those kinds are what they were (tests pin three digests of each kind taken before the tail existed).
- **Invariants** (after the side checks, in this order): `TAIL_FIELD` (gravity out of range, or the pad byte of the field block); per side `TAIL_SIDE` (a side condition out of range); per position `TAIL_POSITION` (a position without a standing occupant, empty or fainted, has no tail at all; for a standing one every range above, the pairs that are zero together, the slots within the occupant's move count, the Substitute within a quarter of its maximum HP, a trap's move within the table and the sources never the occupant's own); per roster member `TAIL_MEMBER` (a member that does not exist has no override; a soak type, a current ability and a toxic stage only on a member standing on the field (a Mega Evolved one can be Soaked, step G11); every id within its kind's table; the toxic stage as above). The decoder adds `TAIL_SCHEMA` (the artifact's schema is not the one of the context's kind: an artifact with a tail under another kind, or a POOL state without one) and `TAIL_RESERVED`; both are reported as MALFORMED with the invariant id, as every decode refusal is (decision 0002 section 6, step 9); an unknown schema, rev 1, rev 2 and rev 3 included, is `SCHEMA_MISMATCH` before any of that. A mechanic that writes the tail clears it where it clears the position (a switch, a faint, a Mega Evolution): `dfi_tail_clear_occupant` clears the position's fields and the occupant's soak type, second type, current ability and toxic stage (the current item and forme outlive the field; a mechanic that gives a temporary forme clears `forme_now` itself), and the invariants catch a miss.
- **Evidence.** `duoforge.state.pool_tail`: the layout asserts; the model's envelope and the 288 bytes of an example with a value in every field; the model's byte sweep (for each of the 288 bytes, how many of the 255 other values decode and with which of the six invariants they are refused, 73,440 decodes); 167 single settings with their own digests, each a valid tail; 97 targeted in-memory edits with their invariant id (the valid edges among them), each also refused by the public check and atomically by a step; every byte of the tail in memory under the other kinds refused; rev 1, rev 2, rev 3 (as genuine artifacts of their sizes, under both POOL kinds and under the other kinds) and every unknown schema refused; every truncation; the capacities; the clearing at the occupant's leaving. The CLOSURE and TEAM_C digests that the test pins (taken before the tail existed) are unchanged. Mutation of the rev 4 implementation, 31 edits (a field not encoded, parsed from another offset or stored short; each kind of reserved byte not checked; rev 3 accepted or written; each invariant dropped or loosened, the hazard permutation included; the second type's clearing dropped or widened): all 31 are caught by this test (a first run left one alive, a separate duplicate test of the hazard order that the check of the kinds makes redundant; it was removed). Mutation of the implementation, 35 edits (a field not encoded, parsed from another offset or stored short; each of the four kinds of reserved byte not checked; rev 1 accepted; each invariant dropped or loosened; a clear of the occupant dropped or widened): 33 are caught by this test, and two are not and cannot be: the Substitute stored as one byte (equivalent: a Substitute is at most a quarter of a maximum HP, and at level 50 no maximum HP reaches 1024, so it fits a byte) and the toxic stage allowed off the field (redundant until the status Tox exists: the status rule refuses the stage first).
- **Byte ledger of the reserve and of rev 5 (both build lanes, 2026-10-09; one table that both lanes read and update).**
  - **Rev 4 reserve.** 35 bytes: field +1..+7; in each position the last 4 bytes (+32..+35); in each member byte +9. It is fully placed:
    - field +1..+6: lane A G46 `party_order`, the `side.pokemon` permutation of both sides;
    - field +7: free;
    - position +32..+35: decision 0028 `transform_forme` (u16) and `transform_pp` (u16);
    - member +9: decision 0028 `transform_source`, the transformed member's own byte, cleared when it leaves.
    Transform needs nothing from rev 5.
  - **Rev 5 (0x0503).** One revision for both lanes, appended after the rev 4 tail. The size is fixed at 60 bytes.
    - **Per side, 18 bytes** (decision 0026, Illusion; on the holder's side): `ill_shown`, `ill_override[4]`, `ill_snapshot[9]`, `ill_pending[4]`.
    - **Per position, 2 bytes** (lane A):
      - `slot_pending`: bit 0 is Healing Wish; the other bits are zero.
      - `future_sight`: 2 bits of turns, 3 bits for the source, 3 bits spare.
      - The step that lands first fixes the semantics.
    - **16 bytes of general reserve**: zero, and refused otherwise (`TAIL_RESERVED`).
    - Whichever lane merges first takes 0x0503 and defines the whole 60-byte layout, with every block not yet used kept zero and refused otherwise. The other lane builds on that layout without changing the size.
    - **Rev 5 is in place** (entry 5aq, step I1 of the Illusion decision 0026): the layout above, every byte zero and refused otherwise until its step writes it. Schema 0x0403 is refused since then.
- **Schema 4 comes only with the certified pool teams** (roadmap "Later"): a certification of the M8 teams is when the state layout is frozen into one schema for every kind; until then the tail keeps CLOSURE and TEAM_C states and their certified evidence untouched. The tail may be revised (0x0503, and so on) until then; rev 2 (0x0203) replaced rev 1 (0x0103) on 2026-10-02 for the view fields of decision 0018, rev 3 (0x0303) replaced rev 2 on 2026-10-03 for the Protect variants of step G20, and rev 4 (0x0403, owner decision of 2026-10-03: cut B of the rev 4 proposal, +40 bytes) replaced rev 3 for the fields of the next state-dependent mechanics. Illusion's view and Revival Blessing's request are later and need no byte of this revision beyond `ability_state`.

## 8. Alternatives considered

- **Generate the whole legal pool once, with a fixed fingerprint.** The row format must grow first (the move flags byte is full; the family parameters are missing), and 510 moves would be encoded before any of them is tested. Rejected at the time; **done as step 7** (section 4.2, owner request of 2026-10-02) once the columns existed and with the UNMODELED rule, so that no row is encoded as if it were tested. The fingerprint still changes with every pool row change.
- **Schema 4 for every kind now** (the pool tail in every state). Every CLOSURE and TEAM_C golden, digest and certified battle would change, and the evidence of decisions 0006 to 0010 would no longer name the bytes it was recorded on. Rejected until the pool teams are certified.
- **Grow TEAM_C's extended tables.** The TEAM_C fingerprint would change with every step, and Team C's gate evidence would no longer name its data. Rejected.
